yadyena rūpeṇārthakriyāsu nopayujyate na tat tadrūpam |142 yathā bījaṃ kuñjaratvena kiñcid apy akurvan na kuñjarasvarūpaṃ tathā ca śālyādayaḥ sāmagrīpraviṣṭā bījatvenārthakriyāsu nopayujyanta iti vyāpakānupalabdhiḥ prasaṅgahetuḥ tadrūpatāyā arthakriyāṃ prati yogyatayā vyāptatvāt anyathātiprasaṅgāt || 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 106. 10-14=C: 121. 1-5)] 
 tadrūpatvam etasya pratyakṣasiddhatvād aśakyāpahnavam iti cet astu tarhi viparyayaḥ yadyadrūpaṃ tat tena rūpeṇārthakriyāsūpayujyate yathā svabhāvena sāmagrīniveśino bhāvā bījajātīyāśraite kuśūlasthādaya iti svabhāvahetuḥ tadrūpatvamātrānubandhitvād yogyatāyāḥ ||143 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 107. 12-18=C: 121. 6-122. 9)] 
 tataś cāsti kiñcit kāryaṃ yatra bījatvena bījam upayujyate iti144 bījānubhava evāsādhāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ yatra bījatvaṃ prayojakaṃ tac ca sarvasmād eva bījād bhavatīti kim anupapannam iti cet na145 yaugikatadanubhavasya tadantareṇāpy upapatteḥ | 
laukika iti cet na satyam etat na tv idam avaśyaṃ sarvasmād bījād bhavati indriyādipratyāsatter asadātanatvāt asārvatrikatvāc ca ||146 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 108. 13-109. 3=C: 122. 9-124. 1)] 
 tataś ca yogyam api sahakāryasannidhānān na karotīty arthasiddham147 kāryāntaram evātīndriyaṃ sarvabījāvyabhicāri bhaviṣyatīti cet tan na tāvad upādeyam amūrtasya mūrtānupādeyatvāt paridṛśyamānamūrtaghaṭitatayā mūrtāntarasya taddeśasyānupapatteḥ148 nāpi sahakāryaṃ mithaḥ sahakāriṇām avyabhicārānupapatteḥ ||149 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 110. 13-16=C: 125. 5-8)] 
 api caivaṃ sati prayojakasvabhāvo nānvayavyatirekagocaraḥ tadgocaras tu na prayojakaḥ dṛśyaṃ ca kāryajātam adṛśyenaiva svabhāvena kriyate dṛśyena tv adṛśyam eveti so 'yaṃ yo dhruvāṇīty asya viṣayaḥ ||150 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 111. 17-112. 3=C: 126. 5-127. 2)] 
 athavā vyatirekeṇa prayogaḥ151 vivādādhyāsitaṃ bījaṃ sahakārivaikalyaprayuktāṅkurādikāryavaikalyaṃ tadutpattiniścayaviṣayībhūtabījajātīyatvāt |152 yat punaḥ sahakārivaikalyaprayuktāṅkurādikāryavaikalyaṃ na bhavati na tadevam bhūtabījajātīyaṃ yathā śilāśakalam iti ||153 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 114. 1-4=C: 128. 9-129. 1)] 
 na ca kim uktasādhyavyāvṛtter uktasādanavyāvṛttir udāhṛtāt kiṃ vā paramparayāpi tathāvidhaprasavasāmarthyavirahād iti vyatirekasandeha iti vācyam154 prāg eva śaṅkābījasya nirākṛtatvād iti ||155 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 115. 1-7=C: 129. 8-130. 6)] 
 syād etat | 
mā bhūt sāmarthyāsāmarthyalakṣaṇaviruddhadharmasaṃsargaḥ astu bījatvam eva prayojakaṃ bhavatu ca sahakārisamavadhāne sati kartṛsvabhāvatvaṃ bhāvasya tathā ca tadasannidhāne 'karaṇam apy upapadyatām tathāpi tajjātīyamātra eveyaṃ vyavasthā na tv ekasyāṃ vyaktau karaṇākaraṇalakṣaṇaviruddhadharmasaṃsargasya pratyakṣasiddhatayā tatra durvāratvād iti cet na virodhasvarūpānavadhāraṇāt ||156 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 117. 9-11=C: 132. 1-)] 
 sa khalu dharmayoḥ paramparābhāvarūpatvaṃ vā syān nityatvānityatvavat dharmiṇi tadāpādakatvaṃ vā śītoṣṇatvavat tadvattā ca157 daṇḍitvakuṇḍalitvavat ||158 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 118. 14-18=C: 133. 5-135. 1)] 
 na prathamaḥ nirviśeṣaṇasyāsiddhatvāt159 yāvat sattvaṃ kiñcit karaṇāt saviśeṣaṇasya tu virodhasiddhāv apy adhyāsānupapatteḥ | 
yadā yadakaraṇaṃ hi tadā tatkaraṇasyābhāvo na tv anyadā tatkaraṇasya na caitayor ekadharmisamāveśamātiṣṭhāmahe ||160 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 120. 13-121. 2=C: 135. 14-137. 2)] 
 na dvitīyaḥ bhāvābhāvavyatiriktayoḥ karaṇākaraṇayor asiddheḥ |161 vyāpārāparavyapadeśasahakāribhāvābhāvau hi karaṇākaraṇe kāryabhāvābhāvau veti162 atirekasiddhāv api svakāla eva svabhāvapratikṣepavat akaraṇābhāvam ākṣipet karaṇaṃ na tv anyadā163 na hi yo yadā nāsti sa tadā svabhāvaṃ pratikṣeptum164 arhatīti virodhyabhāvaṃ vā ākṣeptum | 
tathā sati na kadāpi tan na syāt na vā kadāpi tadvirodhau165 bhaved iti |166 nāsato vidyate bhāvo nābhāvo vidyate sata ity āyātaṃ na vā virodhaḥ ||167 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 123. 12-124. 5=C: 138. 12-140. 6)] 
 nanv evaṃ sati parimāṇabhedo 'pi kālabhedena na viruddhyate168 tatrāpy evaṃ vaktuṃ sukaratvāt169 na170 bādhakabalena tatra kālabhedasya vivakṣitatvāt |171 tathāhi nārabdhadravyair eva dravyāvayavair dravyāntaram ārabhyate mūrtatvasamānadeśatvayor ekadā virodhāt |172 tathā cārambhapakṣe pūrvadravyanivṛttiḥ anivṛttāv anārambha iti |173 tatra nivṛttāv āśrayabhedād eva parimāṇabhedaḥ anivṛttau saṃyogidravyāntarānupacaye kva parimāṇabhedopalambho yo virodham āvahet |174 tadupacaye tu kva parimāṇāntarotpattiḥ āśrayānutpatteḥ |175 ata eva sthailyātiśayapratyayo 'pi tatra bhrāntaḥ tasmāt kālabhedenāpi na parimāṇabhedaḥ ekasmin dharmiṇy upasaṃhartuṃ śakyata ityādi padārhtacintācaturaiḥ saha vivecanīyam ||176 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 128. 4-12=C: 142. 14-146. 2)] 
 astu tarhīhāpi bādhakaṃ balaṃ prasaṅgatadviparyayor uktatvād iti cet na177 tayoḥ sāmarthyāsāmarthyaviṣayatvāt tatra ca uktatvāt178 stāṃ vā na tathāpi tābhyāṃ śaktyaśaktyor avivakṣitatvāt kālabheda eva virodhaḥ sādhyate tathopasaṃhartum aśakyatvāt179 yadā tadety upekṣya yat samarthaṃ tat karoty evety upasaṃhartuṃ śakyam iti cen na180 kālaniyamāvivakṣāyāṃ yat samarthaṃ tat karoty eveti kadācit181 syāt tathā ca sambhavavidher atyantāyogo viruddho na tv ayogaḥ nīlaṃ sarojaṃ bhavaty evetivat ||182 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 132. 8-133. 3=C: 148. 1-)] 
 nanu yadasamarthaṃ prathamam āsīt tasya sāmarthyaṃ paścād api kuta āgatam prathamaṃ samarthasya vā paścāt kutra gatam | 
naitad evaṃ183 tattatsahakārimatas tattatkārakatvaṃ hi sāmarthyam atadvatas tadanyavato vā tadakartṛtvam asāmarthyam | 
idaṃ cotpattikam184 asya rūpaṃ te ca sahakāriṇaḥ svopasarpaṇakāraṇavaśād bhinnakālā ity arthāt kāryāṇām api bhinnakālateti185 tathāpy ekakālastha eva bhāvo jātanaṣṭas tadā tadā tatkāryaṃ karotu utpannamātrasya tatsvabhāvatvāt ekadeśasthavad iti cet ||186 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 135. 11-19=C: 150. 3-151. 4)] 
 seyam ekakālasthatā svarūpāpekṣayā sahakārisānnidhyāpekṣayā vā | 
ādye na kiñcid anupapannaṃ nityānām apy evaṃrūpatvāt vartamānaikasvabhāvatvāt sarvabhāvānām |187 tadeva tu kvacit sāvadhi kvacin niravadhaīti viśeṣaḥ | 
sāvadhitve 'pi vyāpāraphalapravāhaprakarṣāpakarṣābhyāṃ viśeṣaḥ | 
dvitīyas tu syād api yadi teṣāṃ yaugapadyaṃ bhavet kramiṇas tu sahakāriṇa ity uktam188 sahakārisahitaḥ svabhāvena karotīti vaktari tu jātanaṣṭa eva karotv ity uttaraprasaṅgo nirargalaśaiśavasyety alamanena ||189 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 138. 4-12=C: 153. 1-156. 3)] 
 tasmāt kāryasya sa eva kālaḥ kāraṇasya tu sa cānyaś ceti sambandhikālāpekṣayā pūrvakālatāvyavahāraḥ | 
api ca yadā tadeti sthāne yatra tatreti prakṣipya tayor eva prasaṅgatadviparyayoḥ ko doṣaḥ | 
na kaścid iti cet tarhi deśādvaitaṃ vā kāraṇabhedo vā āpadyeta | 
āpadyatāṃ tadādāya yogācāranayanagaraṃ pravekṣyāma iti cet na190 hetuphalabhāvavādavairiṇam191 anapodya tatra praveṣṭum aśakyatvāt | 
tadapavāde vā sattvākhyasādhanaśastrasaṃnyāsinas tava vahirvādasaṅgrāmabhūmāv api kuto bhayam ||192 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 143. 15-18=C: 159. 4-160. 1)] 
 nanu yāvat yo 'rthakriyā bhinnadeśās tāvad bhedaṃ kāraṇam astu ko virodha iti cet na193 teṣām api pratyekaṃ tatprasaṅgrasya tadavasthatvāt194 evam ekasya jagati vastutattvasyā 'lābhe sādhvī kṣaṇabhaṅgasādhanapariśuddhiḥ ||195 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 144. 17-20=C: 161. 1-162. 2)] 
 astu tarhi kaścid doṣa evānayor iti cet sa punaḥ kasmin sādhye196 kiṃ sāmarthyāsāmarthyoḥ kiṃ vā tadviruddhamārdhyāsena bhede āhosvit śaktyaśaktyor virodhe ||197 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 146. 17-147. 3=C: 163. 14-164. 4)] 
 nādyaḥ sarvatra sāmarthye hi prasahya karaṇāt sarvatrāśaktau kvacid apy akaraṇāt198 sarvadeśasamānasvabhāvatve 'py asya svopādānadeśa eva tatkāryaṃ199 karotīti ayam asya svabhāvaḥ svakāraṇādāyāto na niyogaparyanuyogāvarhatīti cet tarhi sarvakālasamānasvabhāvatve 'pi tattatsahakārikāla eva karotīty ayam asya svabhāvaḥ svakāraṇādāyāta iti kin na rocayeḥ ||200 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 147. 13-15=C: 164. 14-165. 1)] 
 na dvitīyaḥ viruddhadharmādhyāsenāpy abhede bhedavyavahārasya nirnimittakatvaprasaṅgāt201 anaikāntikaś ca hetuḥ kālato 'pi na bhedaṃ sādhayet ||202 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 148. 13-149. 7=C: 165. 10-168. 2)] 
 na tṛtīyaḥ virodhalakṣaṇayoge bādhakasahasreṇāpi virodhasyāpanetum aśakyatvāt ayoge vā tadeva cintyam | 
yadvidhāne yasya niṣedho yanniṣedhe vā yasya vidhānaṃ tayor ekatra dharmiṇi parasparaparīhārasthitatayā virodhaḥ sa ceha nāsti taddeśakāryakāritvaṃ hi taddeśakāryākāritvena viruddhaṃ tadvidhau tasya203 niyamena niṣedhāt na punardeśāntare tatkāryākāritvena tasyāniṣedhāt na hy anyatra tadakaraṇam atatkaraṇaṃ vā tatra tatkaraṇasyābhāvo 'pi tu tatra tadakaraṇam iti cet204 hantaivambhūtavirodhalakṣaṇavyāvṛttir205 bhinnakālaśaktyaśaktyor apīty uktaprāyaṃ tatpratisandadhīthāḥ ||206 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 151. 7-13=C: 168. 2-174. 2)] 
 tasmāt prasaṅgatadviparyayasthitāv api asiddho viruddhadharmādhyāsaḥ | 

 nanu yadekadā yat karoti tadyāvat sattvaṃ tat karoty eva yathā kaścic chabdaḥ śabdāntaram iti prasaṅgo 'stu viparyayas tu yad ekadā yan na karoti tat sarvadaiva tan na karoti yathā śilāśakalam aṅkuram |207 na karoti caikadā kuśūlasthaṃ bījam aṅkuram iti cet tad etaj jātyabhiprāyeṇa vā syāt vyaktyabhiprāyeṇa vā syāt ||208 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 154. 13-16=C: 176. 1-4)] 
 prathame dvayam api anaikāntikam aniyamadarśanāt | 
dvitīye dvayam apy anyathāsiddham ekāntāsāmarthyaprayuktatvād atyantākaraṇasya sāmarthye sati sahakārisannidhiprayuktatvāt karaṇaniyamasya ||209 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 156. 1-4=C: 177. 8-11)] 
 etena yadyatkaroti tattadutpannamātraṃ yathā karmavibhāgaṃ yadutpannamātraṃ yan na karoti tan na kadācid api yathā śilāśakalam aṅkuram iti nirastam | 
atrāpi pūrvavadanaikāntānyathāsiddhī doṣāv iti ||210 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 156. 15-16=C: 178. 7-8)] 
 nāpi tṛtīyaḥ kṛtakatvānityatvāder api parasparābhāvavattāmātreṇaiva211 virodhaprasaṅgād iti ||212 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 157. 8-12=C: 178. 14-180. 2)] 
 astu tarhi tasyaiva tenaiva sahakāriṇā213 sambandho 'sambandhaś ceti virodaḥ214 na215 vikalpānupapatteḥ | 
tathāhi sambandhinaḥ sambandhyantare svābhāvasvāhbāvyaṃ vā viruddhyeta abhāvapratiyogitvaṃ vā tadaivetisahitaṃ vā tatraivetisahitaṃ vā ubhayasahitaṃ vā tathaivetisahitaṃ veti ||216 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 160. 4-15=C: 182. 4-187. 4)] 
 na prathamaḥ anabhyupagamāt | 
na dvitīyaḥ satkāryapratiṣedhāt | 
na tṛtīyaḥ prākpradhvaṃsābhāvayor bhāvasamānakālatvānabhyupagamāt | 
na caturthaḥ sa hi na tāvat sthitiyaugapadyaniyamena sambandhinos tadasiddheḥ217 ita eva tatsiddhāv itaretarāśrayatvaṃ niyamasiddhau hi virodhasiddhiḥ tatsiddhau ca bhede sati niyamasiddhir iti | 
na cānyatas tatsiddhis tadabhāvāt aniyatopasarpaṇāpasarpaṇakāraṇaprayuktatvāc ca sambandhāsambandhayoḥ218 nāpi vināśasya ahetukatvāt ayaṃ virodho 'rthāt siddhyati tasyāpy asiddheḥ219 dhruvabhāvitve tu pakṣyāmaḥ220 | 
nāpi pañcamaḥ na hi tadeva221 taraiva sa eva sahakāryasti nāsti ceti abhyupagacchāmaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 164. 4-18=C: 187. 11-189. 3)] 
 nanu samavadhānaṃ nāma sahakāriṇāṃ dharmaḥ saṃyogo bhavadbhir iṣyate sa ca tebhyo vyatirikto 'vyāpyavṛttiś cety api | 
tathā ca sa eva tadaiva tatraivāsti nāsti ceti222 anatireke sthiravādino vyastāny api bījavāridharaṇidhāmāni tāny eveti tebhyo 'pi kāryotpattiprasaṅgaḥ | 
vyāpyavṛttitve ca sarvatra raktādivibhramaḥ śabdādikāryotpattiprasaṅgaś ca | 
tasmād asaṃyuktebhyo 'nya eva saṃyuktasvabhāvāḥ paramāṇavo jātā ity eva jyāyaḥ | 
naitad evaṃ223 kṣaṇikaparamāṇāv apy asya virodhasya durvāratvāt | 
tathāhi224 pūrvadigavasthitaḥ paramāṇur yathā paradigavasthitena paramāṇunā 'paradigavacchedenāvṛtarūpa225 utpannaḥ tathaiva kiṃ pūrvadigavacchedenāpi na vā ubhayathā vā226 ādye ubhayato [']py anupalabdhiprasaṅgaḥ227 dvitīye tu ubhayato [']py upalambhāpattiḥ228 tṛtīye punaḥ sa eva durātmā virodhaḥ sa eva tenaiva tadaivāvṛto 'nāvṛtaś ceti ||229 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 167. 1-6=C: 189. 4-191. 15)] 
 prakārabhedam upādāyāvirodha iti cet kaḥ punar asau230 digantarāvacchedaḥ |231 yadi hi yaddigavacchedenaiva saṃyuktas taddigavacchedenaivāsaṃyukto [']pi tato virodhaḥ syāt iha tu naivam iti cet hanta saṃyogasaṃyoginor bhedapakṣe 'pi yady evaṃ232 siddhāntavṛttāntaḥ syāt kīdṛśo doṣa iti | 
etena vyatirekapakṣe233 'pi nirastaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 168. 4-10=C: 192. 17-194. 5)] 
 adhikaś ca tatrāśrayahetudṛṣṭāntasiddhau pramāṇābhāvaḥ avastuni pramāṇāpravṛtteḥ pramāṇapravṛttāv alīkatvānupapatteḥ | 
evaṃ tarhy avyavahāre svavacanavirodhaḥ syād iti cet tat kiṃ svavacanavirodhena teṣu pramāṇam upadarśitaṃ bhavati vyavahāraniṣedhavyavahāro234 vā khaṇḍitaḥ syāt aprāmāṇiko 'yaṃ vyavahāro 'vaśyābhyupagantavya iti vā bhavet | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 170. 12-18=C: 196. 1-197. 2)] 
 na tāvat prathamaḥ na hi virodhasahastreṇāpi sthire tasya kramādivirahe vā śaśaśṛṅge vā pratyakṣam anumānaṃ vā darśayituṃ śakyaṃ tathātve vā kṛtaṃ bhautakalahena235, dvitīyas tv iṣyata eva prāmāṇikaiḥ236 avacanam eva tarhi237 prāptaṃ kiṃ kurmo yatra vacanaṃ sarvathaivānupapannaṃ tatrāvacanam eva śreyaḥ tvam api paribhāvaya tāvan niṣprāmāṇike238 'rthe mūkavāvadvakayoḥ239 kataraḥ śreyān | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 172. 5-12=C: 197. 14-200. 3)] 
 evaṃ viduṣāpi bhavatā na mūkībhūya sthitam api tu vyavahāraḥ pratiṣiddha evāsatīti cet satyam240 yathā aprāmāṇikaḥ svavacanaviruddho 'rtho mā prasāṅkṣīd iti manyamānena tvayā241 aprāmāṇika evāsati vyavahāraḥ svīkṛtas tathāsmābhir api pramāṇacintāyām (aprāmāṇiko vyavahāro mā prasāṅkṣīd iti manyamānair ) aprāmāṇika eva svavacavirodhaḥ svīkriyate | 
yadi tūbhayatrāpi bhavān samānadṛṣṭiḥ syād asmābhir api tadā na kiñcid ucyata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 174. 11-16=C: 201. 6)] 
 tṛtīye tv aprāmāṇikaś cāpy avaśyābhyupagantavyaś ceti kasyeyam ājñeti bhavān eva praṣṭavyaḥ | 
vyavahārasya suddaḍhanirūḍhatvād iti cet aprāmāṇikaś ca suddaḍhanirūḍhaś ceti vyāghātaḥ242 kathañ cid api vyavasthitatvād iti cet aprāmāṇikaś cen na kathañcid api vyavatiṣṭhate243 prāmāṇikaś ceti244 tadevocyatām iti vāde vyavasthā | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 176. 4-8=C: 204. 1-5)] 
 jalpavitaṇḍayos tu pakṣādiṣu pramāṇapraśnamātrapravṛttasya na svavacanavirodhaḥ tatra pramāṇenottaram aniṣṭam aśkyaṃ ca245 apramāṇenaiva tūttare svavacanenaiva bhaṅgaḥ madukteṣu pakṣādiṣu pramāṇaṃ nāstīti svayam eva svīkārāt | 
anuttare tv apratibhaiveti | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 177. 7-10=C: 205. 6-9)] 
 yadi ca vyavahārasvīkāre virodhaparihāraḥ syāt asau svīkriyetāpi na tv evaṃ na khalu sakalavyavahārābhājanaṃ ca tanniṣedhavyavahārabhājanaṃ ceti vacanaṃ parasparam avirodhi | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 178. 4-8=C: 206. 1-5)] 
 vidhivyavahāramātrābhiprāyeṇābhājanatvavāde kuto virodha iti cet hanta sakalavidhiniṣedhavyavahārābhājanatvena kiñcid vyavahriyate na vā ubhayathāpi svavacanavirodhaḥ ubhayathāpy avastunaiva tena bhavitavyam vastunaḥ sarvavyavahāravirahānupapatteḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 179. 12-16=C: 206. 5-207. 3)] 
 neti pakṣe sakalavidhiniṣedhavyavahāravirahīty anenaiva vyavahāreṇa virodhāt avyavahṛtasya niṣeddhum aśakyatvāt246 vyavahriyata itipakṣe 'pi viṣayasvarūpaparyālocanayaiva virodhāt na hi sarvavyavahārāviṣayaś ca vyavahriyate ceti | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 180. 18-181. 8=C: 208. 15-210. 4)] 
 yadi cāvastuno niṣedhavyavahāragocaratvaṃ vidhivyavahāragocaratāpi kiṃ na syāt pramāṇābhāvasyobhayatrāpi tulyatvād iti247 | 
bandhyāsutasyāvaktṛtve 'cetanatvādikam eva pramāṇaṃ vaktṛtve248 na kiñcid iti cen na249 tatrāpi sutatvasya vidyamānatvāt | 
na hi bandhyāyaḥ suto na sutaḥ tathā sati svavacanavirodhād |

[ĀtTaViv (B: 183. 1-8=C: 212. 5-215. 1)]

[ĀtTaViv (B: 185. 17-186. 3=C: 216. 8-217. 5)]

[ĀtTaViv (B: 187. 11-20=C: 217. 5-220. 7)]

[ĀtTaViv (B: 252. 13-20=C: 300. 1-302. 1)] 
 nanu ghaṭābhāve ghaṭo 'sti na vā360 ādye ghaṭavati tadabhāvaḥ kapāle ghaṭo 'stīti tāny api tadvanti prasajyeran, nāstītipakṣe 'navasthāprasaṅgaḥ, abhāvāntaram antareṇa tatra nāstitāvyavahāre bhāvāntare 'pi tathā prasaṅgaḥ | 
na361, bhāvāntarasya sasvajātīyatvenāviruddhasajātīyatvāt362 viruddhajātīyasya363 vā samānajātīyatvānupapatteḥ, anyatvamātreṇa tathā vyavahāre tadvaty api prasaṅgāt |

[ĀtTaViv (B: 255. 16-256. 4=C: 304. 9-305. 9)]

[ĀtTaViv (B: 270. 1-9=C: 321. 1-322. 2)] 
 niṣkampapradīpakuṅmaleṣu nipuṇaṃ nibhālayanto 'pi na viruddhadharmasaṃsargam īkṣāmahe, atha ca pratyabhijñānam avadhūya tatra bheda eva padaṃ vidhatta iti cet kasya pramāṇasya balena |

[ĀtTaViv (B: 326. 15-18=C: 386. 10-387. 2)]

[ĀtTaViv (B: 327. 9-19=C: 387. 12-389. 3)] 
 etena bhedād dharmiṇaḥ pratītāv api śabdaliṅgadvārā dharmāṇāṃ ced apratītiḥ, indriyadvārāpi mā bhūd ityādikaṃ tu karṇasparśe kaṭicālanam apāstam | 
tattadupādhyupalambhasāmagrīvirahakāle prasañjitasyeṣṭatvāt | 
vicitraśaktitvāc ca pramāṇānām, liṅgasya prasiddhapratibandhapratisandhānaśaktikatvāt, śabdasya samayasīmavikramatvāt, indriyasya tv arthaśakter apy apekṣaṇāt | 
na tu sambaddho 'rtha ity eva pramāṇaiḥ pramāpyate, atiprasaṅgāt | 
yasya tūpādher upalambha eva yena pramāṇena423 dharmyupalabhyate tasyānupalambhe sa tena nopalabhyata iti paraṃ yujyate, sarvopādhyanupalambhe vā, tathā ca siddhasādhanam iti saṅkṣepaḥ ||424 

[ĀtTaViv (B: 330. 6-20=C: 391. 8-393. 5)] 
 syād etat | 
yadīndriyeṇa samānaviṣayāv eva liṅgaśabdau, tataḥ pratibhāsabhedo 'nupapannaḥ | 
ekaviṣayatvaṃ hi pratibhāsābhedena vyāptaṃ savyetaranayanadṛṣṭavat dṛṣṭam, na ceha tathā, yathā hi pratyakṣe cetasi deśakālāvasthāniyatāni parisphuṭarūpāṇi svalakṣaṇāni pratibhānti, na tathā śabde laiṅgikavikalpe [']pi | 
tatra hi vijātīyavyāvṛttam iva parasparākārasaṅkīrṇam ivāsphuṭam iva pratyakṣāparicitaṃ kiñ cid rūpam ābhāsamānam anubhavaviṣayaḥ, na copāyabhedamātreṇa pratibhāsabheda upapadyate, na hi pratipattyupāyāḥ pratipattyākāraṃ parivarttayitum īśate, na caikaṃ vastu dvyākāram iti pratibandhasiddhiḥ | 
asya prayogaḥ, yo 'yaṃ kvacid vastuni pratyakṣapratibhāsād viparītaḥ pratibhāso nāsau tenaikaviṣayo yathā ghaṭagrahāt paṭapratibhāsaḥ, tathā ca gavi pratyakṣapratibhāsād viparītaḥ pratibhāso vikalpakāla iti | 


[ĀtTaViv (B: 332. 17-333. 7=C: 394. 11-396. 3)]

yenākāreṇaikaviṣayatvaṃ tayor na tenaiva virodho yena ca virodho na tenaikaviṣayatvam |425 dharmāntarākāreṇa virodho nīlamātrākāreṇa caikaviṣayateti cet, nanv ihāpi dharmāntarākāreṇa virodho gotvavatpiṇḍamātrākāreṇa caikaviṣayateti tāvanmātranirākaraṇe 'siddho hetuḥ | 
pūrvatra siddhasādhanam |

[ĀtTaViv (B: 351. 1-17=C: 415. 9-420. 1)]

gṛhītād agraho bhedasyāgṛhītebhyas tu tadgraha iti cet, yadi dharmalakṣaṇo bhedaḥ, tadā viparyayaḥ |

ādye apratipattir vā syāt, aviśeṣāt, niḥsvarūpapratipattir vā syāt, ubhayathāpi sāmānādhikaraṇyapravṛttī na syātām | 
dvitīyas tu prāgeva dūṣitaḥ |

tādātmye katham anekatvam | 
ekatve [']pi kva tatkṛto [']pi bhedādhyavasāyaḥ | 
ullekho [']yam asya vijñānasya yad anekatvaṃ nāma, na punastattvāntaram iti cet, svarūpam asvarūpaṃ veti vācyam | 
ādye anekasvarūpam ekam iti ko 'nyā vadedasusthāt | 
dvitīyas tv iṣyata evāsmābhiḥ, kiṃ tu tvayeṣyatām | 
bhrāntir asāv iti cet, tasyām api svarūpam asvarūpaṃ vā prakāśeta, prakārāntarābhāvād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 467. 11-18] 
 syād etat | 
yathā 'tat tattayā parisphurad api na vastugatyā tad eva, tathā tad apy atattayā parisphurad api nātad eva, tataḥ svarūpasyātattayā prathane [']pi na kācit grāhyalakṣaṇakṣatir iti cet, yadyatattāpi parisphurantī svarūpam evāsya syāt, syād apy asyopanyāsasya prastutopayogaḥ | 
asvarūpasya kathaṃ prakāśa iti cet yady api tathā vakṣyāmaḥ, tathāpy atattā kathaṃ tasya svarūpam itīto [']pi dīyatāṃ dṛṣṭiḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 469. 19-470. 8] 
 kaś cāsvarūpasphuraṇe doṣaḥ | 
nāyaṃ bhinnayor vedyavedakabhāvo vyāpāranibandhano niyatasya tasyābhāvāt, nāpi tajjātinibandhanaḥ kaścitkañcitpratijñeya iti vyavasthānupapattiprasaṅgāt, na hi nisargasiddhānāṃ gotvādīnām īdṛśī rītir iti cet | 
nanv ayam abhede [']pi doṣas tadavastha eva | 
tathā hi svasya vedanam iti nāyaṃ vyāpāranibandhano vyavahāraḥ svātmani tadabhāvāt, nāpi jātinibandhanaḥ sādhāraṇyaprasaṅgāt, na hi jātyaiva gauḥ svātmanaṃ prati na tu paraṃ pratīti niyamo dṛṣṭaḥ, na ca jñānaṃ svasyeva parasyāpi vedanaṃ sarvasarvajñatvāpatteḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 472. 13-17] 
 na tat kasyacit, kiṃ tu vedanamātram iti cet, tathāpi svātmanyajaḍavat paratrāpy ajaḍaṃ syāt, parasminn andhavat svasminn apy andhaṃ prasajyeta, jātyaiva tadrūpatvāt | 
jātyāpi svakāraṇāt kvacinn iyatarūpam eva tadutpannam iti cet, naivam uccair brūyāḥ, parair api kadācid evaṃ śrūyeta | 


[ĀtTaViv 475. 1-3] 
 abhedo 'stu mā vā, bhedaṃ tu prakāśamānatvena vyāsedhāma iti cen na | 
vastuni bhedanivṛtter evābhedarūpatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 475. 13-17] 
 astu tarhi bhedābhedavidhuram eva citram, ceto [']bhede prakāśamānatvāyogāt, abhede citratvanupapatter iti cen na, mitho 'viruddhavidhidvayavidhivat tadubhayaniṣedhasyāpy ekatra viruddhatvāt | 
na ca so 'py astv iti vācyam | 
syād vādāvatāre tavāpi digambaratvaprasaṅgāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 477. 14-19] 
 astu tarhi catuḥśikharīśūnyam eva citram, āścaryarūpatvāt, ekānekatvavirahe [']pi sattvam ity āścaryārtho hi citraśabda iti cet, atha catuḥśikharaśekharam eva kiṃ na syāt, āścaryarūpatāyuktes tulyatvāt, ekaikapakṣānupapatiś ca yathā tanniṣedhaparyavasāyinī tathā taditaravidhiparyavasāyiny api syād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 479. 12-17] 
 api cātra vastutaś catuṣkoṭivirahe cetaso bhāṣāntareṇedam uktam, yad anātmāna evaitāś catasraḥ koṭiyo bhāsante na vā pratibhāntīti | 
tatrāpratibhāsanam anuttaram | 
pratibhāsane tu grāhyalakṣaṇāyoge [']pi grāhyabhāva iti citram etat | 
tathā ca citrādvaitādvaraṃ citradvaitam astu pratibhāsanānurodhād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 481. 1-12] 
 syād etat | 
bahirantarubhayathāpi grāhyalakṣaṇakṣatir astu, prakāśamānatvaṃ tu nīlādīnām aśakyāpahnavam, tāvanmātraṃ cāsmākam abhimatam iti cet, tad etad vikrītagavīrakṣaṇam | 
kim idaṃ (hi) prakāśamānatvaṃ yat sarvathā grāhyalakṣaṇakṣatāv api na kṣīṇam | 
na prakāśasambandhaḥ, niyamānupapatter ity uktam | 
tasmān nīlādīnāṃ prakāśamānatvaṃ paripālayatā grāhyalakṣaṇe yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ pariharttavyaṃ vā prakāśamānatvam | 
anyathā tapanīyam apanīya vāsasi granthikartāram upahasasi, svayaṃ ca kanakam upādāya gaganāñcale granthiṃ karoṣīti | 
seyaṃ sarvaprakāram asiddhiḥ sarvaprakāraṃ cānaikāntikatvam iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 484. 17-485. 3] 
 etena dvitīyaḥ pakṣaḥ pratyuktaṃ | 
na hi sajātīyatvena grāhyalakṣaṇavaidhuryam utsāryate, atiprasaṅgasya tādavasthyāt | 
jaḍatve prakāśāsambhāvanaiveti cen na, tulyatvāt |yathā hy asvasamvedanavādinaḥ paraṃ prati prakāśam api jñānam ātmani jaḍam eva, tathā svasamvedanavādino [']pi svātmani prakāśam api jñānaṃ paraṃ prati jaḍam eva | 
kathañcit budhyantare [']py ajaḍaṃ cet, bāhye [']pi tathā kiṃ na syād iti sandigdhavipakṣavṛttitvam | 


[ĀtTaViv 486. 17-487. 3] 
 nanu bāhye sarvathaiva grāhyalakṣaṇakṣatiḥ, iha tu samānopādānatāniyamena citrākārāṇāṃ paramārthabhinnānām eva vyativedanasiddhiḥ | 
na | 
mithaḥ prathāniyame hy upādānamukhena sāmānyato vā sāmagrīsāmarthyam eva vaktavyam, anyathā tathāvidhakāryānutpatteḥ, tathā ca bāhyagrāhyaniyatasvabhāvajñānotpattāv api sulabham etad iti pūrvaka eva doṣaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 489. 5-9] 
 astu tarhi tṛtīyaḥ, sarvathā grāhyalakṣaṇānupapatteḥ, tadabhāve sāmagrīsāmarthyāpy āśrayitum aśakyatvāt, vicārasiddhe hi vastuni kāraṇacintanāvasaro na tv avivecita iti cet, kiṃ grāhyalakṣaṇānirvaktavyatayā prakāśamānatvam amīṣāṃ nivarttate sattvaṃ vā | 


[ĀtTaViv 490. 16-18] 
 na prathamaḥ | 
na hi lakṣaṇāparijñānamātreṇa spaṣṭadṛṣṭam api lakṣyam apahnotuṃ śakyate, aparijñānasya dūrūhatvenāpy upapatteḥ, teṣām apratibhāse tanniṣedhasyānupapatteḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 491. 16-19] 
 na dvitīyaḥ, tadā hi tallakṣaṇānupapattiḥ sattvaṃ nivarttayet, yady asattve lakṣaṇam upapadyeta, ubhayathāpy anupapattau ko 'nurāgo 'sattve | 
tad eva lakṣaṇam iti cet, sattvam eva kiṃ na syāt | 
atiprasaṅgād iti cet, tulyam | 


[ĀtTaViv 492. 11-18] 
 vedanādhīnavyavahāragocaratvam iti cet, astu tāvad idam, tasyaiveti tu niyamaḥ kutaḥ | 
sāmagrītas tathā vedanotpattir iti cet, tad etat sambhāvyate sati, na tv asatīti viśeṣaḥ | 
yathā hi sati jñānenābhilāṣaḥ, tena yatnaḥ, tena pravṛttiḥ, tayā tatprāptiḥ kriyate, na tathā alīke tasya prāptum aśakyatvāt, śakyatve vā 'nalīkatvāt | 
vyavahāro [']py ayam alīka iti cet, tarhi sutarāṃ lakṣaṇābhāvaḥ, taddvārasyāpy abhāvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 494. 8-13] 
 astu tarhi sarvathā vicārāsahatvam eva viśvasyeti cet, tatkim idānīṃ tattvopaplava eva, kāṣṭhāśūnyatā vā | 
na prathamaḥ, iyatīṃ bhūmimārūḍhasyāpi vicārasya niścalatāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt, bhāve vā kathaṃ tattvopaplavaḥ, asyaiva vicārasyānupaplavāt, tatsamānanyāyasyānyasyāpi tathābhāvaprāpteḥ, niṣphalatvāc ca | 


[ĀtTaViv 496. 8-22] 
 na hy asyātipravṛttiḥ phalam, gaganāsvādanenātṛpteḥ, jvālā(alīka)kalapāliṅganena tāpānapanodanāt | 
nāpy atinivṛttiḥ, jaḍībhāvamātre [']py upanipātiduḥkhānivṛtteḥ | 
na ca duḥkham api vicārāsaham ity aheyam eva, tathāvidhasya vicāre [']py anadhikārāt | 
nāpi yathālokaṃ vyavasthitiḥ, vicārāt prāg api tasyāḥ pāmarādivadayatnasiddhiḥ | 
nāpi paralokamātrān nivṛttiḥ, tasyaihikatulyatvāt | 
dṛśyate hi tāvad ayam iti cet, yadi jñānavacano dṛśis tadāparo [']pi tathā | 
sākṣātkāravacanaś cet, ihaivānumānāder apravṛttiprasaṅgaḥ | 
tad api pratyakṣam iti cet, āmuṣmikam api pratyakṣam eva, tathā 'pi kiṃ vicāreṇa, tamantareṇāpi tasyāḥ sulabhatvāt, tām evāyaṃ puṣṇātīti cet tulyalakṣaṇatvāt | 
arucer eva tatra na pravṛttir iti cet, aviśeṣād ihāpi puṣṇīyād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 499. 10-17] 
 so 'yaṃ pavanatanayavārtām upaśrutya tatparddhayā bālavānaraḥ kiyad api dūram utplutya mahārṇave patitaḥ prāha, apāra evāyam akūpāro mithyā ramāyaṇam iti, tat kim anena | 
evaṃ jñātuṃ nirvaktuṃ vā na śakyate kīdṛśaṃ jagad ity etāvanmātram api pāmaradaśāvan niṣphalam eva | 
na ca niṣphalatve [']pi śraddheyam idaṃ, tāvarparāmarśapāṭavābhāvenāpy upapatteḥ | 
na hi jātyandho nīlaṃ jñātuṃ nirvaktuṃ vā na śakta ity ajñeyānirvācyam eva talloka iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 501. 9-17] 
 astu tarhi śūnyataiva paramaṃ nirvāṇam iti cen na, sā hi yady asiddhā, kathaṃ tadavaśeṣam api viśvam abhidhīyate, vāṅmātrasya sarvatra sulabhatvāt | 
parataś cet siddhā, paro 'py abhyupagantavyo grāhyalakṣaṇaṃ cāvarjanīyam iti | 
sa ca paro yadi saṃvṛtir eva, viśvaśūnyatayor na kaścid viśeṣaḥ, kathaṃ tad apy avaśiṣyeta | 
asaṃvṛtirūpaś cet paraḥ, parata eva tasya siddhāv anavasthā | 
svayam asiddhaś cet, kathaṃ śūnyatvam api sādhayet | 
svataḥ siddhaś cet, āyāto 'si mārgeṇa | 


[ĀtTaViv 503. 15-504. 12] 
 tathā hi | 
svataḥ siddhatayā tadanubhavarūpam, śūnyatvād eva ca na tasya kālāvaccheda iti nityam, ata eva ca na deśāvaccheda iti vyāpakam, ata eva tannirdharmmakam iti vicārāspṛṣṭam, tasya dharmmadharmmibhāvam upādāya pravṛtteḥ, ata eva viśeṣābhāva ity advaitam, prapañcasyāpāramārthikatvāc ca, niṣpratiyogikam iti vidhirūpam, avicāritaprapañcāpekṣayā tu śūnyam iti vyavahāraḥ | 
tathāpi prapañcaśūnyasyānubhavamātrasya prapañcena saha kaḥ sambandhaḥ, na ca nāyaṃ prakāśata iti cet, vastugatyā na kaścit, saṃvṛttyā tu gaganagandharvanagarayor ādhārādheyabhāva iva viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ, sa ca yathā naiyāyikaiḥ samarthayiṣyate tathaiva, vedyaniṣṭhas tv asāv asmin darśana iti viśeṣaḥ | 
avidyaiva hi tathā tathā vivarttate yathā yathā 'nubhāvyatayā vyavahriyate | 
tattanmāyopanītopādhibhedāc cānubhūtir api bhinneva vyavahārapathamavatarati gaganam iva svapnadṛṣṭaghaṭakaṭāhakoṭarakuṭīkoṭibhiḥ | 
tadāstāṃ tāvat, kim ārdrakavaṇijo vahitracintayā | 


[ĀtTaViv 508. 1-8] 
 tasmād anubhavavyavasthitau anātmāpi sphuratīty avarjanīyam etat | 
tatsiddhau tallakṣaṇam api kiñcid asty eva | 
tac ca bāhyāvirodhe jñānānityatāyāṃ tanniṣṭham ubhayaniṣṭhaṃ vā, bāhyavirodhe jñānaniṣṭham eva, tannityatāyāṃ tu grāhyaniṣṭham eveti | 
tathā hi, lakṣyas tāvad atra viṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ | 
sa ca prakāśasya satas tadīyatāmātrarūpaḥ svabhāvaviśeṣaḥ | 
svabhāvatvād eva ca nopakārāntaram apekṣate, tanmātrīyatvād eva ca nānyadīyaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 517. 5-16] 
 kim asya phalam iti praśnaprasaṅge [']pi bahis tadgocaravyavahārapravṛttir āntaram api tena jñānanirūpaṇam | 
kuto 'yam īdṛg ity anuyoge [']pi sāmagrīśaktir evottaraṃ kāryakāraṇavat | 
anyathā tatrāpy upakārāntarāpekṣāyām anavasthā, tadanapekṣāyāṃ (vā) sādhāraṇyaṃ kena vāryam | 
tena tad eva kriyate, anenāpy etad eva viṣayīkriyata iti vivecanīyam | 
viṣayīkriyata iti ko 'rthaḥ, kriyata ity api ko 'rtha iti vicāraṇīyam, kāryotpādanam eva karaṇaṃ, tadīyatayotpattir eva viṣayīkaraṇam iti vivecanīyam | 
upakāras tu yathā 'tra vijñānasvarūpātirikto nāsti, tathā 'trāpi kāryasvarūpātirikto nāstīti pratisandheyam iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 519. 7-12] 
 ata eva kāryakāraṇabhāvo 'py upekṣitavya iti tu mahatsāhasam, tathā sati hi syād eva na syād evety ādyāpadyeta, tathā ca prakāśatamasor anyatarasya kauṭasthyaprasaṅgaḥ | 
sāṃvṛto 'stv iti cet, viṣayaviṣayibhāvo 'py evam astu, yadi viṣayakāryayor balavatī bādheti sāṃvṛtaḥ noced evam iti dvayī gatiḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 522. 1-8] 
 tad atra tattvam | 
na tāvad ubhayaniṣṭhaṃ lakṣaṇam, sto 'sato vā sthirasyāpi sphuraṇāt | 
na ca jñānanityatāyāṃ pāramārthikabāhyaniṣṭham satkāryavādavyāvṛtteḥ sāṅkhyaprakriyāvidhvaṃsāt | 
na khalu pūrvvāpara(pūrva)tirobhāvāvirbhāvāv antareṇa vicārasyāpy avasaraḥ | 
nāpy alīkagrāhyatayā kṣaṇikajñānaniṣṭham iti, tucchasya viśeṣābhāvāt, jñānasya ca nirākāratvāt, anyatrāpy a(pra)siddher āropayitum aśakyatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 524. 12-19] 
 asanta eva viśeṣakāścakāsatīti cen na, asanta iti hy atatkālā ataddeśā iti vā akiñcidrūpā iti vā niyatavyāvṛttimātrarūpā iti vā | 
prathame kāladeśāntarayoḥ sattvaprasaṅgaḥ | 
dvitīye tv aviśeṣatā | 
tṛtīye nīlasyālīkasyānīlavyāvṛttirūpatāyām anīlānāṃ pāramārthikatvaprasaṅgaḥ, teṣām alīkatve tadvyāvṛttyātmano nīlasyānalīkatvapattiḥ | 
tathāpy alīkatve 'viśeṣatvam || 

[ĀtTaViv 526. 1-3] 
 tena rūpeṇāviśeṣatvam eveti cet, atha viśeṣaḥ kena | 
nīlapītādineti cet tat kiṃ tato 'dhikam? analīkaṃ tarhi syāt | 
anadhikaṃ cet kutas tenāpīti || 

[ĀtTaViv 526. 18-527. 9] 
 vāsanāvaśād viśeṣāḥ sphurantīti cet, sphurantu, kaḥ kāraṇe vipratipadyate, te tu nīlādayo yady akiñcidrūpāḥ, kathaṃ viśeṣāḥ, taditararūpeṇākiñcidrūpatve tena rūpeṇa kiñcidrūpā eveti brūmaḥ | 
vicārāsahatāmātram alīkatvam iti cet, tathāpi bhāṣāparivarttanamātram, vicārāsahatāyāḥ kiñcidrūpatvavirodhitvāt, avirodhe vā tvadvicārāsahanā api kiñcidrūpā eva nīlādayaḥ iti vicārasya durvicāratvaprasaṅgaḥ tena nīlādīnāṃ chāyāmātrasyāpy anākrānteḥ, praviśa vā anirvacanīyakhyātikukṣim, tiṣṭha vā matikarddamamapahāya nyāyanayānusāreṇa nīlādīnāṃ pāramārthikatve || 

[ĀtTaViv 529. 1-5] 
 tasmāt, 

na grāhyabhedam avadhūya dhiyo 'sti vṛttis 
tadbādhane balini vedanaye jayaśrīḥ | 

no cedanindyam idam īdṛśam eva viśvaṃ 
tathyaṃ tathāgatam atasya tu ko 'vakāśaḥ ||1|| 

[ĀtTaViv 530. 8-531. 6] 
 tatrālīkaṃ vicārāsahamanirvacanīyaṃ vā yam āśritya jagad udgīyate sa eva vicāraś cintyatāṃ ko 'sau kīdṛśaś ceti | 
satarkaṃ pramāṇam eva vākyārūḍham iti cet, tac ced vicārāsahaṃ, kiṃ tena bhautavicārakalpena | 

 tathā hi, kenacid bhautena rājadvāri dviradamavalokya vikalpitam, kim ayam andhakāro mūlakamatti, āhosvit jalavāho balākān varṣati garjati ca, yad vā bāndhavo 'yaṃ, "rājadvāre śmaśāne ca yas tiṣṭhati sa bāndhava" iti paramācāryavacanāt, atha vā yo 'yaṃ bhūmau dṛśyate tasya chāyeti | 

 dūṣitañ ca, tatra nādyaḥ, śūrpayugalaprasphoṭanānupapatteḥ | 
na dvitīyaḥ, tasya stambhacatuṣṭayābhāvāt | 
na tṛtīyaḥ, tasya laguḍabhrāmaṇābhāvāt | 
na caturthaḥ, tasya naraśiraḥśatodgiraṇābhāvāt | 
tato na kiñcid idam iti | 

 kim etāvatā dviradasvarūpaṃ nivartatām, yad vā varam etasmād yo [']yaṃ so [']yam iti vādī dvitīyo bhautaḥ | 
tadvicārasaham iti cet, evaṃ tarhi svayam eva vilīnam alīkādivādaiḥ, nottarapratīkṣaṇam api | 


[ĀtTaViv 533. 1-12] 
 lokasiddham iti cet, tarhi tasya parikaraśuddhir api tathaiva grāhyā, anyathā lokasyāpi vyatikrame vicārasya yādṛcchikavāṅmātravāpatteḥ | 
loke cākāṅkṣāyogyatāsattimattayā pratisaṃhitam aśliṣṭārthaṃ pramāṇāntarāpratihataṃ svavacanasvakriyāsvajñānavyāghātādidoṣarahitaṃ svārthapratikṣepakayukter anākṣepakaṃ vākyam arthapratipatter aṅgam, yathā pūrvato 'yaṃ vahnimān iti | 
anaṅgam itarat, yathā 'yaṃ pūrvato gaura iti, jaladrado vahnimān iti, girir devadattena bhuktam agnimān iti, śveto ḍittho dhāvatīti, śaśo viṣāṇīti, mātā vandhyeti, ahaṃ mūka iti, imaṃ na jānām iti, mama karṇe praviśya gajo garjati bheṣajam ucyatām iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 537. 19-538. 16] 
 tadarthaś ca sādhanaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ ca, tatra sādhanaṃ vyāptipakṣadharmataupayikarūpapañcakopetaṃ liṅgam, yathā viśiṣṭadhūmavattvād iti | 
liṅgābhāsam itarat, yathā jalāśayatvād iti | 
tatparikaraś ca tarkaḥ | 
so [']pi vyāptibalam ālambyāniṣṭaprasaṅgarūpaḥ | 
aniṣṭaṃ ca dvividhaṃ prāmāṇikaparityāgo 'prāmāṇikaparigrahaś ca, yathā yad udakaṃ pipāsāduḥkhaṃ na śamayet na pīyet, yadi ca tad eva paramantarddahet tadāciśiṣṭaṃ mām api dahet | 
itaras tu tarkābhāsaḥ | 
yathā yadi jalaṃ pipāsāduḥkhaṃ nāśamayiṣyat rūpavad api nābhaviṣyat, yathā ākāśam, yadi ca tad eva paramantaradhakṣyat mām api surabhimakariṣyad iti | 
dūṣaṇam api svapratikṣepakayukter anākṣepakaṃ dūṣyāsādhakatvāvinābhūtaṃ siddhaṃ ca | 
yathāgnimattve sādhye prameyatvam anaikāntikatvād ahetur iti | 
anyathā tu tadābhāse, yathā 'yaṃ dhūmo nāgnisādhakaḥ sarvathānupalabhyamānopādhiśaṅkāgrastatvāt prameyatvāt viruddhatvāc ceti | 
itarad api pramāṇam anumānacchāyayaiva vicārāṅgaṃ, tarkam ananyathāsiddhiṃ ca puraskṛtya pravṛtteḥ, tatas tatrāpy eṣaiva rītir anugantavyeti | 


[ĀtTaViv 541. 1-8] 
 evaṃ vyavasthite lokavyavahāre sādhanopakrameṇa yadi vicārayasi, pratijñaiva tāvan nāṅgaṃ dhārayati | 
tathāhi | 
na kiñcid asti na kiñcitsatyaṃ na kiñcitkārakaṃ na kiñcidvicārasahaṃ na kiñcitsālambanaṃ na kiñciddarśanaṃ na kaścitsiddhānta ityādau svārthapratikṣepas tāvad duruddharaḥ | 
pratijñāpi hi na syān na satyā na kārikā na vicārasahā, tadvijñānam api na sālambanaṃ, tatphalam api na darśanaṃ, tadartho [']pi na siddhānta ity āpadyeta || 

[ĀtTaViv 542. 8-13] 
 iṣyata evedam iti cen na, icchāmātreṇa vyāghātānivṛtteḥ | 
yadi hi na (jñātaṃ) kiñcid astītyādipratijñārthaḥ pratijñāṃ spṛśet katham ayam arthaḥ pratyetavyaḥ | 
na cet kathaṃ sānupapannā | 
tadanupapannatve ca kathaṃ punaḥ pratijñārtha upapadyate | 
tad idam āyātaṃ svaviṣamūrcchitā bhujaṅgī ātmānam eva daśatīti || 

[ĀtTaViv 543. 11-17] 
 tattvata iti viśeṣaṇād ayam adoṣa iti cet, tadvicāro vā syāt lokamaryādātikramo vā | 
prathamaḥ pūrvam eva nirastaḥ, tasya pratijñārūpatayā kulīrasyeva svaprasūtayuktyapatyenaiva pratihatatvāt | 
dvitīye tu svacchāyātikramavat sa eva vyāghātaḥ | 
yadi hi lokamaryādātikramo na vicārasvarūpasthitiḥ tatsvarūpasthitiś cen na tadatikrama ity arthaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 544. 17-545. 9] 
 pratiṣedhyasiddhyasiddhivyāghātaś ca | 
yadi hi pratiṣedhyaṃ siddhaṃ nātyantāya pratiṣedhaḥ | 
na cenn atarām | 
lokavyavahārasiddham iti cet, siddham eva hi tarhi, na hy anyato [']pi kiñcit siddhyati | 
nāyam abādhyo vyavahāra iti cet, na, yadi naikatra, anyatrāpi tathābhāvaprasaṅgāt | 
sarvatra bādhyata iti cet, tadapekṣasiddhinā vyavahāreṇānapekṣeṇa vā avyavahāreṇaiva veti | 
yas tāvat tadapekṣasiddhiḥ sa kathaṃ tam eva bādheta, na hy anuṣṇatvānumānenoṣṇatvagrāhipratyakṣabādho loke | 
dvitīyas tv asambhavī, na hi niṣedhakaṃ pramāṇaṃ niṣedhyasiddhinirapekṣaṃ bhavituṃ kṣamate | 
tṛtīye tu tadviparītāpattiḥ, vicārabādhyatvam upakramyāvicāreṇa mudraṇāt || 

[ĀtTaViv 547. 14-17] 
 na caitad api nirvyūḍham | 
sa hy ayatnasiddham adhyakṣaṃ vā syāt aniṣṭimātraṃ vā | 
ādye virodho 'siddhiś ca | 
dvitīye lokātikramaḥ, parāniṣṭyā tvadaniṣṭibādhaprasaṅgaś ceti || 

[ĀtTaViv 548. 12-20] 
 etena hetavo nirastāḥ | 
te hi jñeyatvāt jñāvatvāt asattvāt vikalpānupapatteḥ pratyayatvāt karmānupapatteḥ mitho vyāghātād ityādayaḥ | 
ete hi sarva eva svārthakriyāpratikṣepikāṃ yuktim ākṣipanti, niṣedhyasiddhiñ cāpekṣante | 
anaikāntikau ca prathamadvitīyau svaphala eva vyabhicārāt, asiddhau tṛtīyacaturthau, anaikāntikaś ca pañcamaḥ pūrvavat, asiddhau vyāghātenānaikāntikaś ca ṣaṭhaḥ, asiddho [']naikāntikaś ca saptamaḥ, sarva eva kālātyayāpadiṣṭā iti || 

[ĀtTaViv 552. 4-18] 
 etena dṛṣṭāntā api visarjanīyāḥ | 
te hi rajjusarpavat tajjñānavat śaśaviṣāṇavat darpaṇamukhavat svapnapratyayavat chedyānupapattau chidāvat mithaḥ śoṣakanirvāpakānalasalilavad ityādayaḥ | 
yena ca tatra sarpasyāsattvaṃ tajjñānasya mithyātvaṃ śaśaviṣāṇasyākārakatvaṃ darpaṇamukhasya vicārāsahatvaṃ svapnajñānasya nirālambanatvaṃ chedyānupapattau chidānupapattiś cāvadhāritāni tenaiva tatra rajjoḥ sattvaṃ asarpajñānasya satyatvaṃ śaśasyānyatra gavāder viṣāṇa eva kārakatvaṃ darpaṇasya vicārasahatvaṃ asvapnajñānasya sālambanatvaṃ chedyaniṣṭhā chidā siddhau jñānaṃ karma copapāditāni | 
tadanupapattau vā sādhyavikalatayā sarva evaite dṛṣṭāntābhāsāḥ | 
antim astu sādhanavikalaḥ | 
siddhāntānāṃ vyāghātaḥ parasparaviraharūpatvalakṣaṇaḥ jalānalayos tu vadhyaghātakasvabhāvatvalakṣaṇaḥ, na ca śabdasāmyenānumānam ity eṣā dik | 


[ĀtTaViv 553. 11-19] 
 astu tarhi dūṣaṇopakrameṇa vicāra iti cet, tam api paśyāmaḥ kīdṛśo 'sāv iti | 
nedaṃ sthūlaṃ viruddhadharmmāsaṃsargaprasaṅgāt, nāsthūlaṃ tathā pratibhāsaprasaṅgāt, na parānapekṣaṃ sadātanatvaprasaṅgāt, na ca vyāpakaṃ niṣkriyatvaprasaṅgāt, nāvyāpakaṃ avidheyatvaprasaṅgād ityādir iti cen na | 
mitho [']virodhamūlaśaithilyeṣṭāpādanānukūlatvaviparyayāparyavasānais tarkābhāsatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 557. 16-20] 
 yathā hi nāyaṃ parvato niragniḥ nirdhūmatvaprasaṅgāt nāpy agnimān tathopalabdhiprasaṅgāt ity anayor eka ābhāsaḥ, parasparārthapratikṣepakayor ubhayor anābhāsatvānupapatteḥ, tathātrāpi | 
na hy asthūlatādiniṣedhād anyaḥ sthūlatādividhiḥ tanniṣedhād vānyo 'sthūlatādividhir iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 558. 5-6] 
 śithilamūlāś caite pratibandhasyāsiddheḥ | 
siddhau vā vyāpyavyāpakatadadhikaraṇānāṃ siddhau vivādaniṛtteḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 558. 13-14] 
 iṣṭāpādanaṃ ca prathame, anubhūtāvasite sthūle viruddhadharmāsaṃsargasyeṣṭatvāt | 
tathā ca vakṣyāmaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 559. 4-13] 
 anukūlaś ca dvitīyaḥ | 
asthūlatāpratikṣepasya sthūlatopalambhānugrāhakatvāt tadrūpatāvyavahārasya tathā pratibhāsavyāptatvāc ca | 
evaṃ tṛtīyo [']pi | 
anapekṣatvapratikṣepasya sāpekṣatvopalambhānugrāhakatvāt anapekṣavavidheḥ sadātanatvavyāptatvāc ca | 
caturthas tv aprasiddhavyāpakaḥ, sadasattvasya virodhenaikatra vidhivanniṣedhasyāpy anupapattaḥ | 
pañcamas tv anukūla eva | 
anekatvābhimate vastuny akatāpratikṣepasyānekatopalambhopaṣṭambhakatāt tādrūpyavyavahārasya tathā pratibhāsavyāptatvāc ceti | 
ṣaṣṭhas tv iṣṭa evāsmākam | 


[ĀtTaViv 561. 7-12] 
 nanu bhedaḥ svarūpaṃ vā syād itaretarābhāvo vā dharmāntaraṃ vā | 
na pūrvaḥ, ghaṭo bhinna iti sahaprayogānupapatteḥ | 
nāparaḥ, pratītāvātmāśrayaprasaṅgāt, bhinnapratiyoginirūpaṇād dhi tadabhāvo nirūpyas tannirūpaṇam eva ca bhedanirūpaṇam | 
nottaraḥ, anavasthāprasaṅgāt | 
tat katham asau vyavatiṣṭhatām iti cet | 


[ĀtTaViv 563. 4-13] 
 tat kiṃ bhedajñānam eva nāsti, sad api vā nityaṃ, anityam api vā nirhetukaṃ, sahetukam api vā nirviṣayakaṃ, saviṣayakam api vā bādhyamānaviṣayakam iti | 
tatra prathamaḥ sarvato virodhād anuttaraḥ | 
dvitīyaḥ suṣuptyavasthānurodhād upekṣaṇīyaḥ | 
tṛtīyo [']pi virodhād dheyaḥ | 
caturthas tu bhedollekhād eva tyājyaḥ | 
pañcamas tu cintyate, kim eteṣv anyatam ātmā tasya viṣayaḥ tadanyo veti | 
tatra yady anya eva, kim etabhir vyadhikaraṇānupapattibhis tasya bādhyeta | 
evaṃ hi caurāpaprādhena vyaktam ayaṃ māṇḍasya nigrahaḥ syāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 564. 11-15] 
 athānyatam ātmā, tatrāpi yadi dharmāntaram eveti tattvaṃ, tadā 'navasthābhiyā tadadhika eva pravāhas tyajyatāṃ tasya kutas tyāgaḥ | 
na hy anavasthā pratibhāsamānam arthaṃ nivarttayati, kintu pravāhaṃ parihāpayati, gandhe gandhāntaravat | 


[ĀtTaViv 565. 12-566. 5] 
 athetaretarābhāvam eva bhedajñānam avalambata iti tattvaṃ, tatrāpi kvātmāśrayaḥ, tena hi bhedajñānam eva na syāt, asti ca tat, tato hetvantaram ākṣipet, na tu svātmani svayam ahetutve svayam eva nivartate | 
abidyāvaśād iti cet, kiñcātaḥ | 
na hy avidyety evātmāśrayanivṛttiḥ, tathā sati ghaṭādayo [']pi kulālādinirapekṣāḥ svayam eva bhaveyuḥ | 
athātmāśrayadoṣopahatatayā tan na svasyaiva kāraṇaṃ, tato yataḥ kutaś cit tasya janma, tac ca durnirūpam ato 'vidyety ucyata iti vicārārthaḥ, nāmni tarhi vivādaḥ | 
na ca tad api durnirūpam, pratiyogirūpatvenāpratītāv adhikaraṇapratītir adhikaraṇasvabhāvatvenāsmṛtau pratiyogismṛtiś cetaretarābhāvagrahaṇakāraṇam iti nirūpaṇāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 568. 11-17] 
 atha svarūpam eva bhedapratibhāsaya viṣaya iti tattvam, tathāpi sahaprayoga evānupapannas tyajyatām, bhedena tu kim aparāddham | 
so 'pi dṛśyata iti cet, naimittikas tu syāt, na svarūpataḥ | 
na hi ghaṭamānaya paṭam avalokayetyādau bhedapadam api prekṣāv anupādatte | 
vyākhyāyāṃ tu mūḍhaprabodhanāya ghaṭaḥ kumbha itivat sahaprayoge [']pi na doṣaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 569. 6-16] 
 tathāpi kaḥ paramārthaḥ? yathāyathaṃ trayam api | 
paṭasya hi ghaṭātmanā 'pratītir aghaṭātmanā ca pratītis tato vaiśiṣṭyapratītiś cety anubhavasiddham, tatrābhāvasya, prathamamātram, abhāvāntaradharmāntarayor abhāvāt | 
dravyādiṣu triṣu trayaṃ, trayasyāpi sambhavāt | 
bhavati paṭo 'yaṃ na ghaṭas tantum ayaś ceti, gandho 'yaṃ na rūpaṃ surabhiś ceti,gatir iyaṃ notkṣepaṇaṃ tiryak ceti | 
lakṣaṇaṃ ca svarūpabhedasya tādrūpyeṇāpratītau pratītiḥ, itaretarābhāvasya tv abādhitaḥ samānādhikaraṇo niṣedhapratyayaḥ, vaidharmyasya tu virodhaḥ, sa caikadharmyasamāveśa ity eṣā dik | 


[ĀtTaViv 582. 18-20] 
 saptamo 'py anukūla eva, ghaṭādau vyāpakatāpratikṣepasyāvyāpakatopalambhopaṣṭambhakatvāt, vyāpakatvasya niṣkriyatvavyāptatvāc ca | 


[ĀtTaViv 583. 4-9] 
 aṣṭam astu kvacid iṣṭa eva | 
yata eva hi kvacid avidheyo 'ta eva kvacid vidheyo 'vyāpaka iti vyavahriyate | 
sarvatreti cen na | 
vyaptyasiddheḥ, virodhāc ca | 
na hi yad avyāpakaṃ tat sarvatrāvidheyam iti pratibandhaḥ | 
kvacid astīty avyāpakārthaḥ sarvatra nāstīti cāvidheyārthas tavābhimatas tadanayor virodho 'pi syāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 584. 20-585. 6] 
 yad viṣayasvabhāvaṃ tat kathaṃ pratiṣedhyam iti cen na | 
pratiṣedhasvabhāvatā hi vidheyasya viruddhā, na tu pratiṣedhapratiyogitāpi | 
atha yo [']sti kathaṃ tasya pratiṣedho [']py astīti ce, ko doṣaḥ | 
anayor avirodhaprasaṅga iti cet, prakārabhedena prasa(kto)ṅgo [']pi na doṣam āvahati | 
tadabhedena tu vidhiniṣedhau kena svīkṛtau yaṃ pratyavirodhaḥ prasajyeteti | 
etena kālabhedādinā 'py avirodho draṣṭavyaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 586. 11-12] 
 astu tarhi sthūlaghātī viruddhadharmādhyāso grahaṇāgrahaṇādiḥ pañcavidhaḥ | 
na | 
asiddheḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 587. 3-10] 
 tathāhi | 
yo yena yatraiva yadaivopalabhyate sa tena tatraiva tadaiva nopalabhyata iti nānubhavaḥ, nāpy abhyupagamaḥ | 
ekāvayavasahitasyopalambho 'nyāvayavasahitasyānupalambha iti cet, evaṃ tarhy avayaviny upalabhyamāne kaścid avayava upabhyate kaścin neti vākyārthaḥ, saviśeṣaṇe hi vidhiniṣedhau viśeṣaṇam upasaṅkrāmata iti nyāyāt, tathā cāvayavānām upalambhānupalambhāv avayavini sañcārya prasaṅgas tadalamanena | 


[ĀtTaViv 588. 3-7] 
 etenāvṛtatvānāvṛtatvaṃ vyākhyātam | 
anāvaraṇadaśāvat katipayāvayavāraṇe [']pi tathāvidhasthailyopalambhaḥ kiṃ na syād iti tv avaśiṣyate | 
tad apy asat | 
tasya parimāṇagatasāmānyaviśeṣasya tato 'nyasya niyatasāmagrīvedyasya tadabhāve tadupalambhe [']py anupalambhāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 589. 10-19] 
 etena kampākampasaṃsargo nirastaḥ | 
sāmagrībhedenāvayavaniyatakampotpāde tasyāṃ daśāyām avayavino niścalatvāt | 
evaṃ tarhi kampākampayor avayavayor vibhāgāt saṃyoganāśe dravyanāśaḥ syād iti cet | 
tataḥ kim | 
apasiddhānta iti cen na | 
evam api kvacid abhyupagamāt | 
sarvatraivaṃ prasaṅga iti cet, tathāpy avayavinaḥ kim atyāhitam | 
na caivam api | 
karmaṇo dravyārambhakasaṃyogapratidvandivibhāgajanananiyamānabhyupagamāt | 
kāraṇaviśeṣāt kasya cid eva karmaṇas tathābhūtaviśeṣopalabdheḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 592. 11-593. 6] 
 etena pāṇau calati tanmūlabhūtaḥ paramāṇur api calet, tato 'calattadbhujaparamāṇor vibhāgaḥ, tato yo yena saṃyujyate vibhajyate vā sa tatkāryadravyeṇāpīti nyāyena bhujapāṇyor api vibhāgaḥ, tataḥ saṃyoganāśaḥ, tataḥ śarīranāśa iti nirastam | 

 na hi pāṇiparamāṇukriyā bhujaparamāṇuvibhāgam ārabhate niyamena | 
tadanārambhakatve karmalakṣaṇakṣatir iti cen na | 
ākāśādideśavibhāgajananād api tadupapatteḥ, nudyakarmavat | 
kuto 'yaṃ viśeṣa yady avayavakampe 'py akampa evāvayavī hantāvayavasaṃyogivibhāgibhyām ākāśādideśābhyāṃ na saṃyujyeta na vibhajyeteti cen na | 
avayavasaṃyogavibhāgābhyām eva tatsiddheḥ | 
etac ca samyagavaboddhuṃ vaiśeṣikam anusandheyam | 
tarhi calad avayavasamāśrito 'py acalann evāvayavyupalabhyeteti cet, nedam aniṣṭam, pāṇau kampamāne śarīraṃ na kampata iti pratyayāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 595. 10-596. 3] 
 athavā yo yadāśritatayopalabhyate sa tasmiṃś calatyacalo [']pi cala eva vibhāvyate darpaṇamukhavat jalacandravac cety api draṣṭavyam | 
kṛtaṃ pratītikalahena, evam api sarvatra cala evopalabhyeteti cet, calaty avayave sarvatra tathaiveti na kiñcid anupapannam | 
acale tu kathaṃ tathopalabhyatām | 
tathāpi calācalāśrayasya calācalatayā pratītau calācalajalacandravat dvaitapratyayo [']pi syād iti cet, syād api yadyāśrayavicchedaḥ syāt | 
na hy ekasminn eva jale 'vicchinnāvayavabhedena dvicandrabhramo nāma | 
kintu sa evaikaś cale cala upalabhyate 'cale tv acala iti | 
vīciṣu so [']pi sahasrinima iti cet, evam etat, nāsāṃ vicchedenākalanāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 598. 1-9] 
 astu tarhi calācalayor yutasiddhiprasaṅga iti cen na | 
svayaṃ pratibandhāsiddheḥ | 
na ca prasaṅge vyāptāv api parābhyupagamaḥ śaraṇam | 
na caivaṃ parābhyupagamo [']pi | 
vastrodakādau dṛśyate tāvad ayam iti cen na | 
tantuvastrādau viparyayasyāpi darśanāt | 
idaṃ mithyeti cet, tat kathaṃ satyam | 
abādhād iti cet, iha tarhi bādhakāntaraṃ vācyam | 
calācalatvam eveti cen na | 
yutasiddhāv api kim iti nedaṃ bādhakam iti viparyayasyāpi vaktuṃ śakyatvād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 600. 11-19] 
 raktāraktavirodha iti cen na | 
bhrāntatvāt | 
tanmūlarāgidravyasaṃyogāsaṃyogavirodho 'stv iti cen na | 
paramāṇuvādinaṃ prati prāg eva parihṛtatvāt | 
itaraṃ prati kā vārtteti cet, saiva tāvat tathaivāvirodhāt | 
prakārabhedenāpi virodhābhyupagame vyāpter asiddheḥ saṃyogatadabhāvayor evāsiddheḥ | 
siddhau vā prakārabhedāviruddhasvābhāvasādeśyāparityāgāt | 
na caivaṃ padārthāntaravaidharmyeṇa saṃyoga eva nirākarttavyaḥ, tadvaidharmyeṇa teṣām eva nirākaraṇaprasaṅgāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 602. 17-603. 5] 
 na caivam eva nyāyyam, niyamavatā hi satyavaidharmyeṇetaran nirākriyate, asatyavaidharmyeṇa satyatvasyaiva nirvāhāt, niṣedhyapratītināntarīyakatvāc ca niṣedhasiddheḥ | 
śaśaviṣāṇādau kalpitena niṣedhyena niṣedhasiddhir iti cen na | 
nirākṛtatvāt, saṃyogasya vāṅmātreṇa kālpanikatvasiddhāv atiprasaṅgāc ca, śaśaśṛṇganyāyasya ca yogyānupalambhābhāvenāsiddheḥ, ita eva bādhakāt tatsiddhāv itaretarāśrayatvam | 


[ĀtTaViv 604. 18-605. 9] 
 śabdapratyabhijñānavat saṃyogapratīter anyathopapattimātreṇa bādhakaravṛttir iti cen na | 
anubhavasya tadanurūpam upādhimukhapiṇḍamadatvā bhrāntatvenānyathopapattau tadviparītānumānapravarttate 'gnāv anuṣṇatvānumāne pravṛttiprasaṅgāt | 
na ca pratyabhijñānasya sāmānyavat saṃyogapratyayasya tadanurūpam upādhyantaram asti | 
evambhūtam aryādātikrame tu tvadabhipretapratibandhapratyayasyāpi bhrāntatvenānyathāsiddhiprasaṅgaḥ kathaṃ vāryaḥ, tadavāraṇe śithilamūlas tarkaḥ kathaṃ pravartteta | 
yathālokavyavasthānāṃ cādhyakṣāntaravat saṃyogasyāpi śabdapākajādyarthakriyāsthites tadadhyakṣasyāpi prāmāṇyasiddher na bhrāntatvaśaṅkāvakāśa iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 607. 14-608. 3] 
 astu tarhi taddeśatvāt ataddeśatvarūpo virodhaḥ | 
na | 
virodhalakṣaṇābhāvāt | 
na hi taddeśasaṃsargavidhau niyamena deśāntarasaṃsarganiṣedhaḥ | 
taddeśatvatadadeśatvayos tu syāt, tatsaṃsargas tu keneṣyate | 
adhyakṣam evaikasaṃsargaparicchedakaṃ tadabhāvavyavacchedamukhena tadanyavyavacchedaphalam imaṃ virodham udgiratīti cet, syād apy evaṃ, yadi niyamenaikasaṃsṛṣṭasyānyasaṃsargaṃ pratikṣipadadhyakṣam udiyāt, na tv etad asti, yugapad ekasyānekasaṃsargapravṛttam adhyakṣam aviśeṣāt sarvasaṃsargaṃ vā pratikṣipet, na vā kam api, svātmānam eva vā, sarvathā na virodhaṃ dīpayet, antataḥ paramāṇor apy ekasyānekaiḥ paramāṇubhiḥ saṃsargasvīkārāc ceti | 


[ĀtTaViv 609. 18-19] 
 etena vṛttivikalpo nirastaḥ, paramāṇuvṛttyā 'vavayavivṛttes tulyayogakṣematvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 611. 1-7] 
 astu tarhi citre nīlānīlādivirodhaḥ | 
na hi tad ekaṃ rūpaṃ citratvavirodhāt, nāpy anekam, ekāvayavisamavāyavirodhāt | 
na cānekaṃ vyāpakaṃ, tathānupalambhavirodhāt | 
na cāvyāpkaṃ, svābhāvavideśajātīyatvavirodhāt, anyathā virodhāvirodhavyavasthāvirodhāt | 
na cārūpa evāvayavī, cākṣuṣatvavirodhād iti cen na | 


[ĀtTaViv 612. 14-613. 8] 
 citratvaṃ hi nānātvaṃ vā mithoviruddhanānājātisamavāyaṃ vā 'bhipretya yadi ekatvābhyupagame virodha udbhāvyate, tadaivam etat, na tu tathā 'bhyupagamaḥ | 
na khalv anekatvaṃ citratvam, śukleṣv apy anekeṣu citrapratyayaprasaṅgāt, nāpy ekasmin viruddhānekajātisamavāyaḥ, virodhenaiva nirākṛtatvāt | 
api tu nīlatvādivac citratvam api jātiviśeṣa eva, sa cāvayavavṛttivijātīyarūpasamāhārābhivyaṅgyatvān naikarūpāvayavasahitasyāvayavina upalambhe 'py upalabhyate | 
ata eva tryaṇuke citre [']pi citrapratyayo na kadāpi | 
tathāpy acitre pārśve citrapratyayo mā bhūt, dhavalapratyayas tu kuta iti cet, avayavarūpasañcāreṇāvayavino [']pi tathā pratyayāt | 
ata eva yatrāvayavarūpaṃ na pratyakṣaṃ tatra trasareṇāv etad api nāsti, dṛśyam eva (vā)hyālokarūpam āropya piñjarasvasareṇur ālokyate | 


[ĀtTaViv 615. 16-616. 5] 
 syād etat | 
yadi citratvaṃ nāma jātiviśeṣaḥ, kathaṃ tarhi viruddhajātīyarūpasamāhāramātre citratvapratyaya iti cet, na vai nīladhavalāruṇeṣu paṭeṣu kvacid api kārburārthaś citrapratyayaḥ kasyāpi, vaidharmyanimittas tu kena vāryate? citraśabdasyākṣādpadavad anekārthatvāt | 
nīlapītādiṣu mithaḥ saṃsṛṣṭeṣv anārabdhadravyeṣv api karburapratyayo bhavatīti cet, satyam, dravyāntarotpādābhimānāt sthailyātiśayapratyayavad upapatter iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 617. 5-11] 
 astu tarhi paramāṇunivṛtteḥ sarvavilopaḥ | 
tathāhi | 
bahubhiḥ paramāṇubhiḥ saṃsṛjyamānaḥ paramāṇuḥ pratyekaṃ kim ekadeśena saṃyujyate kārtsnyena vā, prakārāntarābhāvāt | 
na prathamaḥ, tasyaikadeśābhāvāt, bhāve vā paramāṇutvavyāghātāt | 
na dvitīyaḥ, paramāṇvantareṇāsaṃsargaprasaṅgāt, na hy asti sambhavaḥ, ekatraiva parisamāptavṛttir anyatrāpi varttata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 618. 1-13] 
 na | 
buddhyā samānayogakṣematvāt | 
tathāhi | 
buddhir api santam asantaṃ vā 'neka viṣayam ālambamānā ākāraṃ vā bibhratī kārtsnyena vā ekadeśena vā | 
na prathamaḥ, pītādiviṣayākāravilopaprasaṅgāt | 
na hy asti sambhavo nīla eva viṣaya ākāre vā parisamāptātmā pītādisampṛktātmā ceti | 
na dvitīyaḥ, tadabhāvāt | 
evam anākāratvam aviṣayatvaṃ ca buddher iṣyata eveti cet, tat kiṃ yat pratibhāsate tadasat āhosvit na pratibhāsata eva kiñcit | 
nādyaḥ, asaty api nīlapītādau jñānavṛttivikalpasya tadavasthatvāt | 
na hi kṛtsnam eva vijñānaṃ nīlollekhi, pītādyanullekhaprasaṅgāt | 
nāpi tadekadeśaḥ, tadabhāvād ity uktatvāt | 
na dvitīyaḥ, bāhye [']pi vṛttivikalpasyānupapatter iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 619. 7-13] 
 syād etat | 
niḥśeṣasamudāyī kṛtsnaśabdasyārthaḥ, samudāye kaścid eva samudāyyekadeśapadārthaḥ, na ca buddhiḥ samudāyasvabhāvā, tasyā ekarūpatvāt, tat kutaḥ kṛtsnaikadeśavikalpotthānam, kathaṃ tarhi tadviṣayiṇī tadākāravatī vā ? prakārāntarābhāvād iti yadi, tadā svarūpeṇeti brūma iti cet | 
sphuṭaṃ niraṭaṅki tārkikavedikāviṭaṅkena kevalam asmabhyam abhyasūyatā dūraṅgatveti | 


[ĀtTaViv 620. 13-621. 7] 
 etena tadataddeśatvaṃ nirastam | 
tathāhi | 
buddher nīlākāratāṃ paricchindadadhyakṣaṃ tadabhāvavyavacchedamukhena tadavinābhūtāṃ pītādyākāratām api vyavacchindyāt, tathā ca katham ekā buddhir nīlapītādyākārā syād iti tulyo 'nuyogaḥ | 
bhaved evaṃ yadi nīlādyākāratāyāḥ pītādyākāratvābhāvāvinābhāvaḥ syāt, sa eva tu kutaḥ, nīlapītādyākārāyā buddher aikātmyenaiva niścayāt | 
pratyākāraniyatatve citrapratipatter apy anupapattir iti cet, tad etat tulyaṃ paramāṇv avayavyādiṣv api | 
na hi tatrāpy anekaparamāṇusaṃsṛṣṭasya kadāpi na syād iti tulyaivārthagatiḥ | 
vāci vaicityaṃ tu kvopayujyata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 622. 5-6] 
 saṃyogavyavasthāpanenaiva ṣaṭkena yugapadyogāt digdeśabhedācchāyāvṛttibhyām ity ādayo nirastāḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 624. 5-6] 
 paramāṇusiddhyasiddhibhyām eva mūrttatvādayo niravakāśitāḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 625. 3] 
 ākāśavyatibhedādayas tv asambhāvitā eva | 


[ĀtTaViv 625. 16-626. 2] 
 sarvatra cātra hetudaśāyāṃ pratijñāpadayor vyāghātaḥ, kālātyayāpadeśaś ca | 
prasaṅgadaśāyām āśrayāsiddhir vyāptyasiddhiś ca, dṛṣṭāntāsiddheḥ, anavasthālakṣaṇaviparītatarkasya tadupaṣṭambhakatayā truṭerameyatvaprasaṅgasya ca vidyamānatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 627. 15-19] 
 na ca pralayaḥ, paramāṇoḥ, asattvasādhakasya pramāṇasyābhāvāt, sāvayavatvāpādakānāṃ ca hetūnām anavasthotthāpakatvād iti | 
tad anya evāyaṃ paramāṇū raktabījo yasya bhāgā yukticāmuṇḍodaram api bhitvā niṣpatantīti | 


[ĀtTaViv 629. 1-5] 
 na ca kalpitāśrayāḥ kalpitapratibandhāś ca prasaṅgā bhaviṣyantīti yuktam | 
(sve) icchākalpitena vyavahāreṇa sarvavidhiniṣedhavyavahāravilopaprasaṅgāt | 
lokavyavasthāpekṣaṇe tu tadvirodhenottarasya niṣedha(ka)syātmalābhābhāvād ity asakṛdāveditatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 629. 15-630. 3] 
 anyathā sarvam etad buddhāv api samānam | 
sāpi hi ṣaṭkena yugapadyogādibhiḥ sāvayavā prasajyeta | 
tasyām asiddhāsta iti cen na | 
anumānasiddhatvāt | 
tathāhi buddhiḥ ṣaṭkena yugapadyoginī mūrttimatī ca, sattvād vyavaharttavyatvād vā, bodhyavad iti śakyate | 
viparyayasyāpi dharmigrāhakamānasādhitatvād anavakāśam idam iti cet, evam anyatrāpi pratisandadhīthā ity eṣā dik | 


[ĀtTaViv 630. 11-12] 
 anyas tu cakṣuṣī nimīlya nirbhayībhavitum icchann āha, astu tarhi buddher api vilopa iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 631. 3-11] 
 atra tu kiṃ vaktavyaṃ yatra hetvādivyavahāro nāsti | 
astu paraṃ sāmvṛta iti cet, bhaved evaṃ yadi samvṛtir api paramārthasatī syāt | 
anyathā tu yathā na vāstavas tathā na sāmvṛto [']pi | 
samvṛtir api samvṛtisatīti cet, asyā apy asattve na kiñcid adhikam uktam | 
parasamvṛtes tu paramārthasattvasvīkāre saiva buddhir apariheyeti | 
satī ca bādhakavatī ceti samvṛtisatī ceti cet, saty eva yadi tat kathaṃ bādhakam, tathā cet kathaṃ tadālīḍhasya sattvam iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 633. 1-11] 
 dṛśyate tāvad evam iti cet, sattvaikārthasamavāyino bādhakatvam eva tarhi heyam | 
bādhakaikārthasamavāyinaḥ sattvam eva kin na hīyata iti cen na | 
bādhakasyāpi tyāgaprasaṅgāt | 
ubhayam apy avarjanīyam eva tarhi | 
evam etat | 
mitho virodhas tu heyaḥ | 
na hi viruddhayor ekārthasamavāyas tathābhūtayor vā virodhaḥ śakya upapādayitumṛte svasamayāt, na ca vicārāvasare svasamayāvatāra iti | 
yathā yathā ca buddhinivāraṇāya bodhavyam eveti | 
tad etad āyātaṃ pradīpāntareṇa pradīpaṃ nirvāpya timirāpādanam iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 634. 17-635. 3] 
 etena hetuphalabhāvānupapatteḥ sarvavilopa iti nirastam | 
tannirākaraṇaprayāsasya sāphalyavaiphalyābhyāṃ tannirākaraṇānupapatteḥ, satkāryadūṣaṇasyeṣṭatvāt, asatkāyadūṣaṣasya ca pratyakṣabodhitatvāt, tatprati pādanasyāsata evotpattāv anaikāntikatvāt sattve vā prayāsavaiphalyād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 636. 14-17] 
 syād etat | 
mā bhūvann etāni dūṣaṇānyavayavini, anupalambhas tu syāt, na hi paramāṇusañcayādaparaṃ kiñcid upalabhyata iti cet, vailakṣyaviṣṭambhikeyam, sthūlaikānubhavasya sarvajanasiddhatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 637. 7-12] 
 vikalpamātraṃ tad iti cen na | 
spaṣṭapratibhāsatvāt | 
aupādhikam asya spaṣṭatvam iti cet, tathābhūtānubhavam antareṇopādher apy abhāvāt | 
anyathā nīlādivikalpānām api tathaiva spaṣṭatvopapattau sarvapratyakṣocchedaprasaṅgāt, gṛhītaniścita evārthe pratyakṣaprāmāṇyāt, niścayopaplave tasyāpy upaplavād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 639. 3-10] 
 na ca paramāṇava eva sthūlāḥ, tattvavyāghātāt | 
na ca tatsamudāyas tathā, tasya samuditasthānasya tvayā 'nabhyupagamāt, abhyupagame vā 'vayavinā kim aparāddham | 
na ca samuditā eva tathā pratibhāsitum arhanti, teṣāṃ pratyekam asthūlatvāt | 
na ca nānādigdeśavyāpitaiva sthaulyam, paramāṇuṣu pratyekam asambhavati | 
na ca nānātvaikārthasamavayiny eva sā sthaulyam iti sāmpratam, sthūla eka iti pratyayaniyamāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 640. 13-17] 
 na ca bhāgeṣv evāropitenaikatvenedam upapattim ata, tadasambhavāt | 
na hi karacaraṇacibukanāsikādiparamāṇūnām aikyaṃ kaścit kvacid āropayati | 
na ca teṣu bhedena prathamāneṣu na sthūlaprayayaḥ | 
na ca tattve sphuraty eva tadviparītātattvasamāropasambhavaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 641. 9-18] 
 api caivam ekaparamāṇvātmanā paramāṇukoṭir apy āropitā paramāṇumātratayaiva parisphuret, na sthūlatayā | 
na ca nānādigdeśavyāpitvasyaiṣa mahimeti sāmpratam, virodhāt | 
yadi hi nānādikkāḥ paramāṇavo deśatayā 'vabhāseran naikatayā deśitayā cāropyeran, tathā ca kasya nānādigdeśavyāpitā, deśino 'parisphūrtteḥ | 
atha tathātvenāropyeran? na nānātvena deśatvena vā 'vabhāseran, tathā ca kasya nānādigdeśavyāpitā, deśino 'parisphūrtteḥ | 
tasmād ekatvārope paramāṇumātrāvabhāsa eva syād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 643. 12-15] 
 evaṃ tarhy anārabdhadravyarāśiṣu kā vārtteti cet, na tāvaddeśavyāptisāmyena tāvatparimāṇadravyatvāropaḥ | 
na cehāpi tathā syāt, anyatrāpy asiddheḥ | 
na cāsann evaikaḥ sthūlaḥ parisphurati, bādhakānām apāstatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 644. 12-13] 
 etena pratibhāsadharmo 'pi nirastaḥ | 
so 'pi hy asan bauddho vāstavo veti trayīṃ gatiṃ nātivarttata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 645. 9-12] 
 atīndriyāś ca pratyekamaṇavaḥ kathaṃ militā api dṛśyeran, atīndriyasamūhasyāpy atīndriyatvāt | 
viśiṣṭotpādādaindriyakatvam iti cet, kim adyāpi svapne hastaṃ prasārayasi | 


[ĀtTaViv 646. 6-8] 
 astu vaivam, tathāpi kṣaṇabhedāj jātibhedo nirākṛtaḥ, jātisaṅkaraprasaṅgāt | 
tadabhedena tūtpāde 'pi tad vā tādṛgveti tattvadṛśā na kaścid viśeṣaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 647. 4-9] 
 bhavatu vā jātiviśeṣo [']pi tathāpi sthūlatvam evaindriyakatvaṃ prati prayojakaṃ mantavyam | 
anyathā sthūlatāmanāpnuvann eko 'pi paramāṇuḥ kadāpi tathotpannaḥ pratyakṣatāmiyāt | 
niyamena tu tadviparītasya pratyakṣatā tathāvidhasyāpratyakṣatāyām eva viśrāmyati, anvayavyatirekaphalatvād dhetuphalabhāvasya | 


[ĀtTaViv 648. 3-13] 
 na ca sañcitā api sthūlatayotpannāḥ | 
na ca bahutvam eva sthailyaṃ tadviparyaya eva sūkṣmatā, vitatadeśānām api pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgāt | 
nairantaryam api vivakṣitam iti cen na | 
tasya prakṛte 'py asambhavāt, rūpaparamāṇūnāṃ rasādiparamāṇubhir antaritatvāt | 
na cāropyate nairantaryam, itaretarāśrayaprasaṅgāt, nairantaryārope teṣāṃ sthūlānāṃ grahaṇaṃ tadgrahaṇe ca sati nairantaryāropa iti | 
tasmād aindriyakatve sthūlatāyāḥ prayojakatvāt aprayojakatve vitatadeśānām api pratyakṣatvaprasaṅgāt teṣāṃ ca pratyekam asthūlatvād atīndriyā eva paramāṇavaḥ, tathā ca sarvāgrahaṇam avayavyasiddher iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 649. 7-10] 
 astu tarhi kṣaṇamātrasthāyī sthūlo 'rthaḥ iti cen na | 
bhāgabhāginor yugapadupalambhabādhitatvāt ghaṭapaṭādibhaṅge tantukapālādīnām utpāde samānopādānatayā sapratighatvavyāghātāc ceti | 


[ĀtTaViv 651. 11-12] 
 so 'yam adhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyena sthūlatvasiddhau kṣaṇabhaṅgabhaṅgaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 652. 19-653. 3] 
 etena yat sat tan niravayavaṃ yathā vijñānaṃ saṃś ca vivādāspadībhūto ghaṭādir iti nirastam | 
viparyaye bādhakābhāvena vyāptyasiddheḥ | 
yat sat tat sāvayavaṃ yathā ghaṭaḥ sac ca vijñānam iti cārvākaparivarttasyāpy avakāśāc ca | 
samo vā samādhiḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 653. 10-15] 
 nanu niravayavam eva vijñānaṃ svasamviditarūpaṃ, ghaṭasya ca sāvayavatāyām adyāpi vivāda eva, tat kathaṃ parivarttaḥ kathaṃ vā samaḥ samādhir iti cet, śuṣkavivādasya vijñāne 'pi durvāratvāt | 
na hi kaścit kaṇṭhauṣṭhapārśvajaṭharādiparihīnaṃ piṭharam anubhavati | 
astu vā sattvāt sapratighatvasiddhir buddhāv apratighatvaṃ vā ghaṭādāv iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 654. 10-17] 
 api ca svatantrasādhanam idaṃ prasaṅgo vā | 
na prathamaḥ, ghaṭādiśabdena sthūletarāṇāṃ rūpādīnāṃ paramāṇūnāṃ vā pakṣīkaraṇe siddhasādhanāt, sthūlam ekam abhyupagamya pakṣavidhau kālātyayāpadeśāt, anabhyupagame tv āśrayāsiddher iti | 

 nanu prāmāṇike 'bhyupagame bādhaḥ syāt, sarvathā 'nabhyupagame cāśrayāsiddhiḥ syāt, na caivam atreti cet, tad etannabhaḥsthalakamalaparimalasādhanasyāpi sāśrayatām āpādayad āśrayāsiddhidoṣamoṣāyety alamanena | 


[ĀtTaViv 655. 12-656. 3] 
 parvatādival lokapratītisiddhān ghaṭādīnupādāya niravayavatvānumānaṃ syāt, anyathā brahmavivarttādivipratipattivihatatayā dahanānumānam api parvatādau na syād iti cen naivam | 
avirodhāt | 
na hi brahmavivarttādisiddhāv apy āśrayādayo 'nyaprakārāḥ sambhavanti, tathāhi, sarvatra svapnaparvate svapnadhūmena svapnavahnir eva sādhyate, kevalaṃ sāmvṛte [']pi vyavahāre satyānṛtavyavasthā 'stīti tasyāṃ nirbharaḥ karttavyaḥ | 
iha tu ghaṭādivyapadeśena sthūla eva yadi pakṣīkṛtaḥ, kathaṃ niravayavatvena sādhyena na virodhaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 657. 8-18] 
 atha paramāṇur eva, kathaṃ na siddhasādhanam | 
vipratipannaṃ prati na tatheti cet, na vai kaścit paramāṇūnāṃ nirvayavatve vipratipadyate | 
lokavyāmohanibarhaṇāya sādhanam iti cet, tathāpi yaṃ lokaḥ sthūlam ekam upalabdhavān tasya pakṣatve virodha eva, tato 'nyasya pakṣatve siddhasādhanam eva | 
ekaḥ sthūlo 'yam iti mithyeti cet, etad eva tarhi sādhyatām, kim anenājāgalastanakalpena sattvena | 
na ca tatraivedaṃ śakyam upasaṃharttum, vyadhikaraṇatvāt, tasmād yena rūpeṇa yasya pakṣatvaṃ vivakṣitam, tena siṣādhayiṣitadharmavirodhavivādābhyāṃ tatrānumānapravṛttir nāto 'nyatheti | 


[ĀtTaViv 659. 10-15] 
 nāpi dvitīyaḥ, viparyayāparyavasānāt, na ca vivādaviṣayo niravayavas tasmān na sann iti viparyayaḥ, na caivaṃ sāvayavatvaṃ pakṣasyānicchatā pravarttayituṃ śakyate, na ca tat tvayeṣyate, na tasyeṣṭau tenāsattvaṃ śakyasādhanam, āśrayāsiddher virodhād asādhāraṇyād vā, na ca viparyayo [']pi pareṣṭyā pravarttata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 661. 5-7] 
 kaḥ punar avayavini nyāyaḥ | 
tat kiṃ pratyakṣān nyāyo garīyān | 
yady evam, buddhāv eva ko 'sau | 
tasmād asāram etat | 


[ĀtTaViv 661. 17-662. 4] 
 tathāpi yan nirastasamastaviruddhadharmādhyāsaṃ tad ekam eva vyavaharttavyaṃ yathā vijñānam, tathā ca vivādādhyāsita ekasthūlo 'nubhavagocara iti svabhāvahetuḥ, tāvanmātrānuvandhitvād ekatāvyavahārasya | 
na hy ayam ekavyavahāro nirnimitto 'niyamaprasaṅgāt, nāpy anyanimittaḥ, dravakaṭhinaśātoṣṇādāv api tathāvyavahāraprasaṅgāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 662. 14-663. 2] 
 astu tarhi bāhyeśv artheṣu nityasandehaḥ, tathyātathyavibhāgasyāśakyatvād iti cet, na tāvat sarvasya yathārthatvād eva vibhāgo 'nupapannaḥ, uttaravirodhāt | 
tathāhi viparītam avagataṃ mayeti laukikī pratipattiḥ anyathākhyātir iti ca vainayikī yathārthā na vā, ubhayathāpy uttareṇa na sarvayāthārthyasiddhiḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 664. 11-16] 
 śabdasaṃlāpamātram etan na pratītir iti cen na | 
vivādānupapatteḥ, na hi vyavahāramātre parīkṣakāṇāṃ vivādaḥ, na ca nāyam astīti | 
śabdārthe vivāda iti cet, evaṃ satyapratītasya niṣeddhum aśakyatvāt parābhiprāyaniṣedhārthatvāc ca vivādasya, abhyuoagantṛpratiṣeddhroḥ pratiṣedhyapratītiḥ kathaṃ nāstīti | 


[ĀtTaViv 666. 3-12] 
 na cānyajñānād anyatra pravṛttisambhavo 'tiprasaṅgāt | 
na ca tajjñānasānnidhyaṃ niyāmakam atiprasaṅgād eva | 
na ca tatsahito bhedāgrahaḥ, agrahaprādhānye bhedāvivakṣāyāṃ nivṛtter api prasaṅgāt, taddhetor abhedāgrahasyāpi vidyamānatvāt | 
nāsau nivarttakaḥ, api tu bhedagraha iti cen na, rajata eva nedaṃ rajatam iti kṛtvā na nivartteta, bhedagrahasya tatkāraṇasyābhāvāt, bhāve vā viparītakhyātir abhinne bhedapratyayāt, tasmāt pravarttakavan nivarttako [']py agraha eva teṣāṃ svīkarttum ucitaḥ, tathā ca sa doṣas tadavastha eva | 


[ĀtTaViv 669. 5-7] 
 atha bhedaḥ pradhānam, aṅgam agrahaḥ, tadā saty arajatajñānādrajate na pravartteta, idam aṃśarajatāṃśayor bhedābhāvād iti svayam ūhanīyam | 


[ĀtTaViv 670. 11-14] 
 pravṛttivat pratipattāv apy ubhayāgrahas tulya iti cen na | 
agrahasyāvivakṣitatvāt, sāmagrīviśeṣād eva tatsiddheḥ | 
tattve 'parisphuratīti tu niyamaḥ | 
na ca kvacid api bhedābhedāv ubhāv api tattvaṃ yatas tathā syād ity eṣa dik | 


[ĀtTaViv 674. 17-20] 
 nāpi sarvasyāyathārthatvāt, tadgrāhakasya yathārthatvāyathārthatvābhyām uttaravirodhāt | 
tat kiñcit pramāṇaṃ kiñcid apramāṇam iti vibhāga eva vastugatiḥ | 
na cāsau pratītigatim avadhūya vyavahāragocaraḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 675. 17-676. 6] 
 kathantā tu nirūpyate svataḥ parato veti | 
ādye [']pi svayaṃ svagrāhakeṇeti vā | 
tatra na prathamaḥ svasamvedanasyāsiddheḥ, prakāśatvasyāsādhāraṇatvāt, śabdasāmyenānumānāpravṛtteḥ | 
na cādhyakṣam evātra pramāṇam, sandigdhabhedatvāt | 
apratyakṣopalambhasya nārthadṛśṭiḥ prasiddhyati, pareṇa paravedane 'navasthā syāt, na ca kriyā svajātīyakriyākarmabhāvam aśnute cchidāvad iti tarkapuraskārān naiṣa doṣa iti cen na | 


[ĀtTaViv 681. 4-7] 
 upalambhāparaparyāyāyā dṛśṭeḥ siddhir niṣpattir vā syāt pratītir vā | 
ādye 'nāgatopalambhavedanaprasaṅgaḥ, anupalabdhasyāniṣpatteḥ | 
na cāvidyamānenāvidyamānasyopalambha iti svasamvittāv asamvittir eveti | 


[ĀtTaViv 682. 3-12] 
 dvitīye tu siddhasādhanam, na hy anupalabdha upalabdho bhavati | 
na caivaṃ saty artho [']pi nopalabhyeta, na hy upalambhapratītir arthavedanam api tūpalambhaniṣpattiḥ | 
upalambhādṛṣṭāv upalambhaniṣpattir ity eva vyavahāraḥ kuta iti cet, mā bhūt, na hy avyavahārād eva niṣpannasya vastuno nivṛttiḥ | 
tathā ca tannibandhano 'rthavyavahāro durvāra eva | 
na cānavasthā, avaśyavedyatvānabhyupagamāt, niścayavat, anyathā tv aniścitaniścayasyārthaniścayo [']pi na siddhyet, na cāsau svātmany api niścaya iti | 
tad idaṃ vadhūmāṣamāpanavṛttāntam anuharati | 


[ĀtTaViv 685. 6-13] 
 cchidādivad iti tu dṛṣṭāntamātreṇa nāsmad avadhānam | 
jñānaṃ na jñānāntarakarma, tajjātīyakriyātvāt, yā yajjātīyā kriyā nāsau tatkriyākarma, yathā chidā chidāntarasyeti tu nyāyaviplavaḥ | 
chidāvat sarvathā sajātīyākarmatve sādhye bādhitaviṣayatvāt, puruṣāntarajñānasya ca puruṣāntarajñānavedyatvāt, svayam api smṛtyādigocaratvāc ca | 
svakarvatve sādhye dṛṣṭāntasya sādhyavikalatayā hetor viruddhatvād iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 687. 12-14] 
 nāpi svagrāhakeṇa, vivecanānupapatteḥ | 
na hi pramāṇagrahaṇaṃ yathārthatvaikaniyatam, apramāṇe [']pi pramāṇābhimānāt | 
anyathā viparyayajñānād apravṛttiprasaṅgāc ca | 


[ĀtTaViv 690. 1-8] 
 tato yadi prāmāṇyam āpātataḥ sphured api, tathāpi niyamahetor abhāvāt pramāṇam evedam iti niścayaḥ kutaḥ, sa ca mṛgyate, pāralaukikavyavahārāṅgatvāt | 
śaṅkānivarttanena tadapratyūhaṃ niścitam eveti cet, tannivṛttir eva kutaḥ | 
pramāṇāntarād iti cet, tad api niścayaphalam anyathā veti | 
anyathātve na śaṅkāvicchedo niścayasādhyatvāt tasya | 
niścayaphalatve tu yo mṛgyate nāsau svata iti | 


[ĀtTaViv 691. 12-692. 3] 
 sphuraṇamātram api tāvad astu svata iti cet, kiṃ tena | 
na caitad api, ananusaṃhitopādher upahitapratyayāyogāt | 
na ca viṣayopādhānamātraṃ prāmāṇyam, tadābhāsasādhāraṇyāt | 
api tv anubhavasya sato bhūtārthānusandhānam | 
na cārthānusandhāne [']pi tasya bhūtatvam anusandhīyate, āropitatvavyāvarttakaviśeṣaṇānanusandhānāt | 
ananusaṃhitasya cāropasādhāraṇyāt | 
kvacid viśeṣo [']py anusandhīyata iti cet, na kvāpi prathamikena | 
anyathā 'nabhyāsadaśāyāṃ tatrāpi saṃśayo na syāt | 
abhyāsadaśotpanne tu anusandhānaṃ vyāptigrahajanitasaṃskārasamudbhavasmaraṇabalena bhavat pramāṇāntaraśarīra eva praviśatīti parata evāvaśiṣyate | 


[ĀtTaViv 697. 8-698. 2] 
 tatrāpy anavastheti cet, na tāvad asau dṛṣṭāntadvārikā, prāgeva tanniścayāt | 
phaladvārikā tu syād api, yadi prāmāṇyam avaśyaniśceyam abhyupeyate | 
ajñātaprāmāṇyena kathaṃ paraprāmāṇyavedanam iti cet, yathā viṣayasaṃvedanam | 
astu tarhi dharmiliṅgadvārikā, tābhyām aniścitābhyām ananumānāt tanniścayasya ca prāmāṇyaniścayam antareṇānupapatteḥ | 
na | 
dvaitābhāvāt | 
yatra hi liṅgajñāne dharmijñāne vā tathyātathyabhāvena dvaitam upalbhyate tatra tathyatvāniścaye liṅgam ābhāsaśaṅkākrāntatayā na niścīyate, yatra tu tajjñānaṃ tad ekaniyataṃ tatra tāvataiva liṅganiścayaḥ, tadābhāsaśaṅkānutthānāt | 
ekakoṭiniyato hy anubhavo niścayaḥ | 
jñānataddharmagrāhiṇi ca jñāne na dvaitam iti vyavasthitir eva | 
prāmāṇyaniścayas tu tasyāpi parata eveti nyāyasampradāyaḥ | 
ita eva viśeṣāt tādṛśasya svata eveti tātparyācāryāḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 701. 15-702. 4] 
 syān matam | 
etad eva tu kathaṃ niśceyaṃ yad evambhūtam ekakoṭiniyatam eva yāvatā tatrāpy anavasthitir iti cen na | 
vyāptijñānasya sākṣād ātmany apravṛttāv api sarvopasaṃhāreṇa yam upādhim ādāya pravṛttis taddharmavattvāt tajjātīyatvaṃ hi tatropādhis tac ca tatrāpy aviśiṣṭam anuvyavasāyatvāt | 
na ca sāmānyato niyamaniścaye tadāliṅgite viśeṣe dvaitaśaṅkāvakāśaḥ | 
yathā vācakaḥ śabda iti svātmano vācakatvaṃ na sākṣād vidhatte vṛttivirodhāt, tathāpi sāmānyena yam upādhim adhikṛtya pravṛttaḥ śabdas tadvattvāt śabdaśabdo vācako na veti śaṅkayā na paribhūyate nāpy anavasthety evam ihāpīti | 


[ĀtTaViv 703. 19-704. 5] 
 tarkāś cātra bhavanti | 
yadi dharmajñānaṃ vyabhicaret, nirālambanam asadālambanaṃ vā bhavet, niṣiddhaṃ ca tat | 
yady anuvyavasāyo vyabhicāret, tadārthakarmako [']pi na syāt, jñānetarasyārthaprāvaṇyābhāvāt | 
yadi tajjātīyaṃ vyabhicaret avyabhicāro na kvacid vyavatiṣṭheta | 
tadavyavasthitau vyabhicāro [']pi na syāt, anyathākhyātirūpatvāt tasya, tattvasthiau cānyathātvaṃ syāt, tadavadhikatvāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 705. 16-19] 
 etena svapnajāgarāvasthayor aviśeṣa iti nirastam | 
asatkhyāter ātmakhyāteś ca nirākṛtatvāt, anyathākhyāteś ca tattvakhyātivyavasthām antareṇānupapatter iti | 
sā ca jāgare [']pi yadi na syāt, na syād eveti | 


[ĀtTaViv 706. 13-19] 
 tathāpi katham anayor avasthayor vibhāgaḥ karttavya iti cen na | 
lokasiddhatvāt | 
kim anayor lakṣaṇam iti cet, kartṛkarmakaraṇadeśakālaprabandhabādhaḥ kākatālīyasamvādavān svapnasya, prabandhe kākatālīyaḥ kasya cid eva viṣayasya bādho jāgarasyeti | 
etena bādhyaprabandho 'vasthāviśeṣaḥ svapnaḥ prabandhabādhakādir vādhyamānaprabandhānto jāgaritam ity api draṣṭavyam ity eṣā dik | 


[ĀtTaViv 708. 10-15] 
 tasmāt tathyam eva viśvaṃ, mandaprayojanatvāt tu satvarair mumukṣubhir upekṣitam iti yuktam utpaśyāmaḥ | 
tarhi naiyāyikānāṃ jagatparirakṣaṇe ko [']yam abhiniveśātiśaya iti cet, sahasaiva tadupekṣāyāṃ nyāyābhāsāvakāśe pramāṇamātraviplavo bhavet, tathā ca nyāyaruciḥ prekṣāvān na tattvam adhigacched iti bhiyeti |

ṅuṇaguṇibhedabhaṅgavāda 
[ĀtTaViv 710. 2-7] 
 astu tarhi guṇaguṇinor abhedān nairāmyam, kṣaṇikajñānamātrapariśeṣād iti cet, ucyate | 
asti tāvad iha darśanasparśanābhyām ekārthānusandhānam | 
tad idam ekaikaviṣayaṃ vā syāt samudāyaviṣayaṃ vā, tadatiriktaviṣayaṃ vā, vastvananurodhyākāraviṣayaṃ vā, alīkaviṣayaṃ vā | 


[ĀtTaViv 712. 3-7] 
 na tāvad adyaḥ, na hi yad eva rūpaṃ sa eva sparśa iti | 
na ca rūpaṃ tvagindriyagrāhyam, andhasyāpi nīlādipratyayaprasaṅgāt | 
na caikam eva vastu karaṇabhedenānyathā prathata iti yuktam, anātmakatvaprasaṅgāt, bhedābhedavyavasthānupapatteś ca | 


[ĀtTaViv 714. 14-15] 
 nāpi dvitīyaḥ, sa hi ekadeśatayā vā ekakālatayā vā ekakāryatayā vā ekakāraṇatayā veti | 


[ĀtTaViv 715. 4-17] 
 na tāvad upādānarūpaikadeśasambhavaḥ, tayoḥ pratiniyatopādānatvāt, sambhave vā tad eva dravyam iti paryavasitaṃ vivādena | 
nāpy adhikaraṇībhūtabhūtalādyādhāratayā tatsambhavaḥ, cakṣuṣā hy upalabhyamāne bhūtale rūpaviśeṣe ghaṭo [']pi cakṣuṣaivopalambhyamāne bhūtale rūpaviśeṣas tadādhāra iti śakyate niścetum, tayor adharottarabhāvenaikajñānasaṃsargitvāt, tadadhikaraṇāḥ sparśādayo [']pīti tu kasya pramāṇasya viṣayaḥ | 
na hi sparśādayaś cākṣuṣe cetasi cakāsati | 
tvaco 'yaṃ vyāpāra ity api nāsti, tayāpi bhūtalaghaṭasparśayor ādhārādheyabhāvapratīteḥ, na ca samudāyayos tatsambhavaḥ, parasparāśrayatvaprasaṅgāt, ekādhāratāyāṃ hi samudāyānusandhānaṃ samudāyānusandhāne caikādhāratānusandhānasambhava iti | 
anavasthāprasaṅgāc ca, bhūtalasyāpi samudāyatvaṃ kiṅkṛtam ity anuyogānivṛtteḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 717. 8-10] 
 ata eva naikakālatayāpi, tayor ekakālatāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt, bhāve vā rāsabhakarabhayor apy ekakālatayā samudāyatvaprasaṅgaḥ, bhedāgrahasya prakṛte [']py asambhavāt | 


[ĀtTaViv 718. 4-7] 
 ata eva naikakāryatayāpi, upādeyarūpasyaikakāryasyābhāvāt | 
ekodakāharaṇalakṣaṇārthakriyetyādy api na yuktam, rūpādyatiriktasyodakasyānabhyupagamāt, ekaikasyānekāhāryatve pramāṇābhāvāt, samudāyasya cāsiddheḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 719. 1-2] 
 ata eva naikakāraṇatayāpīti | 
nimittam antareṇa tu samudāyavyavahāre 'tiprasaṅgaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 719. 7] 
 tṛtīye na vivādaḥ |

[ĀtTaViv 719. 14] 
 ādye tu rūpādiṣu kaḥ pakṣapātaḥ | 


[ĀtTaViv 720. 1-12] 
 prāpter arthakriyāsthiteś ca na dvitīyo [']pi | 
te dve rūpādīnām iti cet, na teṣām, kintu tasyaiveti kiṃ na syāt |

sambandhābhāva iti cet, asambandhas tāvad astu | 
kathaṃ tadvat pratīyata iti cet, asambandhas tu | 
kathaṃ tadvat pratīyata iti cet, teṣāṃ tathotpādād iti parihāro 'stu tavaiva yathā śarīraṃ cetanāvad iti | 
rūpādibhir eva samastārthakriyāsiddheḥ kiṃ tadatiriktadravyakalpanayeti cen na | 
tāvanmātreṇaiva samastārthakriyāsiddheḥ kiṃ rūpādikalpanayety asyāpi vācāṭavacaso 'vakāśaprasaṅgāt | 
pratīyamānatvād iti cet, tulyam | 


[ĀtTaViv 721. 18] 
 etenālīkaviṣayatāpi nirastā | 


[ĀtTaViv 722. 1-4]

sahopalambhaniyama iti cen na |

 nāsau śaṅkhaḥ, kintu śaṅkha iva, taimirakakeśavad iti cet, aho guṇavad viveṣaḥ, yat taimirikakeśāḥ karatalaparāmarśapratiniyatārthakriyayor apārān na sambhavantīti vyavasthāpayati, iha tu tatsambhave [']pi śubhratāmātrānupalambhād iti |

 syād etat |

dharmavirodha eva ko 'treti cet, tadupalambhe [']py anupalambhaḥ tadabhidhāne [']py anabhidhānaṃ tanniṣedhe [']py aniṣedha ityādi |

[ĀtTaViv 730. 10-16] 
 abhede ca dharmadharmiṇor dūrādūratayā grahaṇe paṭvapaṭunī kutaḥ | 
na ca puruṣabhedena tathaivānyasyotpādaḥ, ekasya dvairūpyābhāvāt |

[ĀtTaViv 732. 12-19] 
 na cādhipatipratyayatvāt tasyeti yuktam, anubhavānāṃ taddeśollekhānanurodhāt, ullekhe vā sa evālambanapratyayaḥ, tāvanmātrānubandhitvādavalambanavyavahārasya | 
na ca deśo 'stv ālambanaṃ na tu vṛkṣādir iti sāmpratam, anupalabdhaviśeṣatayā deśadeśinor aviśeṣāt |

[ĀtTaViv 735. 17-21] 
 aniścayānupapatteś ca | 
anubhūto hy ātmā na niścīyata ity atra hetur vācyaḥ |

[ĀtTaViv 736. 16-737. 3] 
 tathāpi cānubhavakalpanāyāṃ sarvaḥ sarvadā sarvaṃ jānāti na tu niścinotīti kiṃ na syāt |
